function writeToSheetAndExportDoc_PL(data) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(20000); 
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    const sheet = ss.getSheetByName("PLHD");
    if (!sheet) throw new Error("Không tìm thấy sheet PLHD!");

    // 1. GHI SHEET MỒI
    const dateVal = data.date || "Không có ngày";
    sheet.getRange("B3:E3").setValues([[dateVal, data.field8 || "Không có số HĐ", data.field2 || "", data.field9 || ""]]);
    sheet.getRange("BF3:BG3").setValues([[data.selectedBF3 || "", data.field10 || ""]]);
    const blCell = sheet.getRange("BL3");
    blCell.setNumberFormat("@"); 
    blCell.setValue(data.adjustmentIds || "");

    SpreadsheetApp.flush(); 

    const row3Data = sheet.getRange(3, 1, 1, 78).getValues()[0]; 

    // 2. GHI SỔ THEO DÕI
    const targetSheet = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
    const targetRow = getNextRowAfterLastData_SO(targetSheet, 3, 2, 5);

    const jValue = getCurrentStaffName();
    const hasValue = (parseFloat((data.field2 || "").toString().replace(/\./g, '')) || 0) !== 0;

    const valuesToWrite = [[
      row3Data[1], row3Data[51], "", data.field8, "", row3Data[6], row3Data[21], 
      row3Data[31], jValue, row3Data[3], "", row3Data[57], row3Data[59],
      "", "", hasValue ? "x" : "", "", "" 
    ]];
    targetSheet.getRange(targetRow, 2, 1, 18).setValues(valuesToWrite);

    // 3. TẠO VÀ XỬ LÝ DOCS (CÓ BỘ BẢO VỆ QUYỀN TRUY CẬP DRIVEAPP)
    const docTemplateId = "1S02EAglGKBo55f4TT_oMKawDyZcNF-Wl2GZRjO-e5G4";
    const selectedNDs = data.selectedNDs || [];
    const fileName = generateFileName_PL(data.field8);

    let destinationFolder;
    try {
      destinationFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(EXPORT_FOLDER_ID);
    } catch(e) {
      throw new Error("Tài khoản không có quyền truy cập Thư mục đích (EXPORT_FOLDER_ID).");
    }

    let copiedFile;
    try {
      copiedFile = DriveApp.getFileById(docTemplateId).makeCopy(fileName, destinationFolder);
    } catch(e) {
      throw new Error("Không thể nhân bản File Mẫu Phụ lục. Vui lòng kiểm tra quyền truy cập Template ID.");
    }

    // 🚀 BỌC AN TOÀN 1: Bỏ qua lỗi nếu Google Workspace chặn quyền bật Link Sharing Public
    try {
      copiedFile.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW); 
    } catch(shareErr) {
      console.warn("Bỏ qua lỗi setSharing do chính sách bảo mật Workspace chặn: " + shareErr.message);
    }

    const copiedDoc = DocumentApp.openById(copiedFile.getId());
    const body = copiedDoc.getBody();

    const lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
    const dataRange = sheet.getRange(2, 2, 2, lastCol - 1).getDisplayValues();
    const placeholders = dataRange[0];
    const replaceVals = dataRange[1];

    placeholders.forEach((placeholder, i) => {
      if (placeholder && placeholder.trim().startsWith("[")) {
        body.replaceText(escapeSpecialChars(placeholder.trim()), replaceVals[i] || "");
      }
    });
    
    const rawIds = row3Data[63] || ""; 
    const computedIds = typeof computeAllActiveIds === "function" ? computeAllActiveIds(rawIds) : []; 
    const otherSections = row3Data.slice(64, 78).map(v => v.toString().trim()).filter(Boolean);
    
    const validSectionsSet = new Set([...computedIds, ...otherSections, ...selectedNDs].map(String));

    // Dùng hàm chung optimizeDocSections (thay cho vòng lặp cắt block trùng lặp)
    optimizeDocSections(body, validSectionsSet);

    copiedDoc.saveAndClose();

    // 🚀 BỌC AN TOÀN 2: Bỏ qua lỗi nếu Google Shared Drive không cho phép xóa file rỗng
    if (DocumentApp.openById(copiedFile.getId()).getBody().getText().trim() === "") {
      try {
        DriveApp.getFileById(copiedFile.getId()).setTrashed(true);
      } catch(trashErr) {
        console.warn("Bỏ qua lỗi setTrashed do giới hạn quyền Shared Drive: " + trashErr.message);
      }
      return { link: "" };
    }
    
    return { link: copiedFile.getUrl() };

  } catch (error) { 
    throw new Error("Lỗi xử lý Phụ lục: " + error.message); 
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock(); 
  }
}

function updateContractData_PL(maHD, editType, newValue) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(20000); // Khóa luồng 20 giây tránh tranh chấp trượt dòng với lệnh xóa
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    const sheetLog = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
    if (!sheetLog) throw new Error("Không tìm thấy Sổ gốc!");

    const lastRow = sheetLog.getLastRow();
    const dataE = sheetLog.getRange(1, 5, lastRow, 1).getValues(); 
    const maClean = maHD.trim().toUpperCase();
    
    let targetRow = -1;
    for (let i = dataE.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        if (dataE[i][0].toString().trim().toUpperCase() === maClean) {
            targetRow = i + 1;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (targetRow === -1) return false; 

    if (editType === "DATE") {
        sheetLog.getRange(targetRow, 2).setValue(newValue); 
    } 
    else if (editType === "PACKAGE") {
        sheetLog.getRange(targetRow, 9).setValue(newValue); 
    } 
    else if (editType === "VALUE") {
        const num = Number(newValue.toString().replace(/\./g, ""));
        sheetLog.getRange(targetRow, 11).setValue(num); 
    }
    else if (editType === "CONTRACT_NO") {
        sheetLog.getRange(targetRow, 5).setValue(newValue.trim()); 
    }

    SpreadsheetApp.flush(); 
    return true;
  } catch (e) { 
    throw new Error("Lỗi Backend: " + e.message); 
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock(); // Giải phóng khóa
  }
}

/**
 * Xuất dữ liệu ra Google Spreadsheet:
 * - Dùng dấu chấm phẩy (;) cho hàm =SUBTOTAL(9; ...)
 * - Sắp xếp cấp Dự án: Nhiều hợp đồng nhất xếp trên cùng, ít hợp đồng nhất xuống dưới cùng
 * - Sắp xếp cấp Hợp đồng nội bộ: Ngày ký từ cũ nhất đến mới nhất
 * - Hàng cuối cùng chèn "TỔNG GIÁ TRỊ" tính bằng =SUBTOTAL(9; ...)
 */
function exportToNewSpreadsheet_PL(payload) {
  try {
    let filteredA = [];
    let contractsOnly = false;
    let groupByProject = false;

    if (Array.isArray(payload)) {
      filteredA = payload;
    } else if (payload && typeof payload === 'object') {
      filteredA = payload.codes || [];
      contractsOnly = !!payload.contractsOnly;
      groupByProject = !!payload.groupByProject;
    }

    if (!filteredA || filteredA.length === 0) {
      throw new Error("Hệ thống không nhận được mảng dữ liệu xuất tệp Spreadsheet hợp lệ!");
    }

    let titleSS = "DATA HỢP ĐỒNG";
    if (contractsOnly && groupByProject) titleSS = "DATA HỢP ĐỒNG GỐC THEO DỰ ÁN";
    else if (contractsOnly) titleSS = "DATA HỢP ĐỒNG GỐC (CỘNG DỒN PL)";
    else if (groupByProject) titleSS = "DATA HỢP ĐỒNG THEO DỰ ÁN";

    const newSS = SpreadsheetApp.create(titleSS);
    const file = DriveApp.getFileById(newSS.getId());
    const destinationFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(EXPORT_FOLDER_ID);
    file.moveTo(destinationFolder);

    // BẬT CHẾ ĐỘ: BẤT KỲ AI CÓ LINK ĐỀU CÓ THỂ CHỈNH SỬA
    try {
      file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.EDIT);
    } catch (shareErr) {
      console.warn("Bỏ qua cảnh báo setSharing do chính sách bảo mật Workspace: " + shareErr.message);
    }

    const sheet = newSS.getActiveSheet();
    sheet.appendRow(["NGÀY KÝ", "SỐ HỢP ĐỒNG / PLHĐ", "DỰ ÁN", "TÊN NHÀ THẦU", "TÊN GÓI THẦU", "GIÁ TRỊ (VND)", "NGƯỜI THỰC HIỆN"]);
    
    sheet.getRange("A1:G1").setFontWeight("bold").setBackground("#00B050").setFontColor("white").setHorizontalAlignment("center").setVerticalAlignment("middle");
    sheet.setRowHeight(1, 35);

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    const sourceSheet = ss.getSheetByName("X");
    const data = sourceSheet.getRange(`A4:N${sourceSheet.getLastRow()}`).getValues();

    // Tra cứu danh mục Tên dự án từ Sheet DATABCONS
    const bconsSheet = ss.getSheetByName("DATABCONS");
    const bconsData = bconsSheet ? bconsSheet.getRange(3, 1, Math.max(1, bconsSheet.getLastRow() - 2), 2).getValues() : [];
    const projectNameMap = new Map();
    bconsData.forEach(r => {
      const pCode = (r[0] || "").toString().trim().toUpperCase();
      const pName = (r[1] || "").toString().trim();
      if (pCode) {
        projectNameMap.set(pCode, pName ? `Dự án ${pCode} - ${pName}` : `Dự án ${pCode}`);
      }
    });

    function parseDateToTime(val) {
      if (!val) return 0;
      if (val instanceof Date) return val.getTime();
      const s = val.toString().trim();
      const dmy = s.match(/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{1,2})\/(\d{4})/);
      if (dmy) {
        return new Date(parseInt(dmy[3], 10), parseInt(dmy[2], 10) - 1, parseInt(dmy[1], 10)).getTime();
      }
      const ymd = s.match(/(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})/);
      if (ymd) {
        return new Date(parseInt(ymd[1], 10), parseInt(ymd[2], 10) - 1, parseInt(ymd[3], 10)).getTime();
      }
      const parsed = Date.parse(s);
      return isNaN(parsed) ? 0 : parsed;
    }

    // 1. TRÍCH XUẤT DỮ LIỆU
    let extractedRows = [];

    if (!contractsOnly) {
      filteredA.forEach(valA => {
        const matchedRow = data.find(row => (row[0]?.toString().trim() || "") === valA.trim());
        if (matchedRow) {
          const giaTriRaw = matchedRow[2] || "";
          const projCode = (matchedRow[13] || "").toString().trim().toUpperCase();
          extractedRows.push({
            projCode: projCode,
            rowData: [
              matchedRow[7] || "", matchedRow[0] || "", matchedRow[13] || "", 
              matchedRow[10] || "", matchedRow[1] || "", 
              (giaTriRaw === "" || isNaN(giaTriRaw)) ? "" : Number(giaTriRaw), 
              matchedRow[12] || ""
            ]
          });
        }
      });
    } else {
      const uniqueParentCodes = [];
      const seenParents = new Set();

      filteredA.forEach(code => {
        let parentCode = code.trim();
        const plMatch = parentCode.match(/^PL\d+\s*-\s*(.*)$/i);
        if (plMatch && plMatch[1]) {
            parentCode = plMatch[1].trim();
        }
        if (!seenParents.has(parentCode.toUpperCase())) {
            seenParents.add(parentCode.toUpperCase());
            uniqueParentCodes.push(parentCode);
        }
      });

      uniqueParentCodes.forEach(parentCode => {
        const parentUpper = parentCode.toUpperCase();
        const parentRow = data.find(row => (row[0]?.toString().trim() || "").toUpperCase() === parentUpper);
        
        if (parentRow) {
          let totalContractValue = Number(parentRow[2]) || 0;
          data.forEach(r => {
            const rCode = (r[0] || "").toString().trim().toUpperCase();
            if (rCode.startsWith("PL") && rCode.includes(parentUpper)) {
              totalContractValue += (Number(r[2]) || 0);
            }
          });

          const projCode = (parentRow[13] || "").toString().trim().toUpperCase();
          extractedRows.push({
            projCode: projCode,
            rowData: [
              parentRow[7] || "", parentRow[0] || "", parentRow[13] || "",
              parentRow[10] || "", parentRow[1] || "",
              totalContractValue, parentRow[12] || ""
            ]
          });
        }
      });
    }

    // 2. TẠO CẤU TRÚC HÀNG VÀ CHÈN HÀM SUBTOTAL(9; ...)
    const rowsToAdd = [];
    const groupHeaderRowMeta = [];

    if (!groupByProject) {
      extractedRows.sort((a, b) => parseDateToTime(a.rowData[0]) - parseDateToTime(b.rowData[0]));
      extractedRows.forEach(item => rowsToAdd.push(item.rowData));
    } else {
      // Gom nhóm theo mã dự án
      const groupsMap = new Map();
      extractedRows.forEach(item => {
        const key = item.projCode || "KHAC";
        if (!groupsMap.has(key)) groupsMap.set(key, []);
        groupsMap.get(key).push(item.rowData);
      });

      // SẮP XẾP CÁC DỰ ÁN: NHIỀU HỢP ĐỒNG NHẤT XẾP TRÊN CÙNG, ÍT HỢP ĐỒNG NHẤT XUỐNG DƯỚI CÙNG
      const sortedProjectGroups = Array.from(groupsMap.entries()).sort((a, b) => {
        const countDiff = b[1].length - a[1].length; // Sắp xếp giảm dần theo số lượng hợp đồng
        if (countDiff !== 0) return countDiff;
        return a[0].localeCompare(b[0], 'vi', { numeric: true });
      });

      sortedProjectGroups.forEach(([pCode, rowsInGroup]) => {
        // A. Sắp xếp ngày ký từ cũ nhất đến mới nhất cho từng dự án
        rowsInGroup.sort((a, b) => {
          const tA = parseDateToTime(a[0]);
          const tB = parseDateToTime(b[0]);
          if (tA !== tB) return tA - tB;
          return (a[1] || "").toString().localeCompare((b[1] || "").toString(), 'vi', { numeric: true });
        });

        const headerTitle = projectNameMap.get(pCode) || (pCode === "KHAC" ? "Dự án khác" : `Dự án ${pCode}`);
        const headerRowIdx = rowsToAdd.length + 2; // Hàng 1 là tiêu đề cột chính
        const startRow = headerRowIdx + 1;
        const endRow = headerRowIdx + rowsInGroup.length;

        // B. Chèn hàm SUBTOTAL(9; ...) dùng dấu chấm phẩy (;)
        const projectFormula = rowsInGroup.length > 0 
          ? `=SUBTOTAL(9; F${startRow}:F${endRow})` 
          : 0;

        groupHeaderRowMeta.push({
          rowIdx: headerRowIdx,
          title: headerTitle
        });

        // Chèn dòng tiêu đề dự án chứa công thức SUBTOTAL tại Cột F
        rowsToAdd.push([headerTitle, "", "", "", "", projectFormula, ""]);

        // Chèn các hợp đồng thuộc dự án
        rowsInGroup.forEach(r => rowsToAdd.push(r));
      });
    }

    // C. HÀNG TỔNG GIÁ TRỊ TOÀN BỘ CÁC DỰ ÁN: SUBTOTAL(9; ...) dùng dấu chấm phẩy (;)
    let grandTotalRowIdx = 0;
    if (rowsToAdd.length > 0) {
      grandTotalRowIdx = rowsToAdd.length + 2;
      const grandTotalFormula = `=SUBTOTAL(9; F2:F${grandTotalRowIdx - 1})`;
      rowsToAdd.push(["TỔNG GIÁ TRỊ HỢP ĐỒNG TRƯỚC VAT", "", "", "", "", grandTotalFormula, ""]);
    }

    if (rowsToAdd.length > 0) {
      sheet.getRange(2, 1, rowsToAdd.length, 7).setValues(rowsToAdd);
    }

    const lastDataRow = sheet.getLastRow();
    if (lastDataRow >= 2) {
      const fullRange = sheet.getRange(1, 1, lastDataRow, 7);
      fullRange.setVerticalAlignment("middle")
               .setFontFamily("Arial")
               .setFontSize(11)
               .setWrap(true)
               .setBorder(true, true, true, true, true, true, "#000000", SpreadsheetApp.BorderStyle.SOLID);
      
      sheet.getRange("A2:A" + lastDataRow).setHorizontalAlignment("center");
      sheet.getRange("B2:B" + lastDataRow).setHorizontalAlignment("left");
      sheet.getRange("C2:C" + lastDataRow).setHorizontalAlignment("center");
      sheet.getRange("D2:E" + lastDataRow).setHorizontalAlignment("left");
      sheet.getRange("F2:F" + lastDataRow).setNumberFormat("#,##0").setHorizontalAlignment("right");
      sheet.getRange("G2:G" + lastDataRow).setHorizontalAlignment("center");

      // 3. ĐỊNH DẠNG DÒNG TIÊU ĐỀ DỰ ÁN VÀ CỘT F
      groupHeaderRowMeta.forEach(item => {
        const rIdx = item.rowIdx;
        sheet.getRange(rIdx, 1, 1, 5)
             .merge()
             .setValue(item.title)
             .setFontWeight("bold")
             .setBackground("#D9E1F2")
             .setFontColor("#1F4E79")
             .setHorizontalAlignment("left");

        sheet.getRange(rIdx, 6)
             .setFontWeight("bold")
             .setBackground("#D9E1F2")
             .setFontColor("#1F4E79")
             .setNumberFormat("#,##0")
             .setHorizontalAlignment("right");

        sheet.getRange(rIdx, 7).setBackground("#D9E1F2");
        sheet.setRowHeight(rIdx, 30);
      });

      // 4. ĐỊNH DẠNG HÀNG "TỔNG GIÁ TRỊ" CUỐI CÙNG
      if (grandTotalRowIdx > 0) {
        sheet.getRange(grandTotalRowIdx, 1, 1, 5)
             .merge()
             .setFontWeight("bold")
             .setBackground("#C6E0B4")
             .setFontColor("#274E13")
             .setHorizontalAlignment("left");

        sheet.getRange(grandTotalRowIdx, 6)
             .setFontWeight("bold")
             .setBackground("#C6E0B4")
             .setFontColor("#274E13")
             .setNumberFormat("#,##0")
             .setHorizontalAlignment("right");

        sheet.getRange(grandTotalRowIdx, 7).setBackground("#C6E0B4");
        sheet.setRowHeight(grandTotalRowIdx, 32);
      }
    }

    const colWidths = [110, 350, 75, 500, 800, 170, 170];
    colWidths.forEach((width, index) => {
        sheet.setColumnWidth(index + 1, width);
    });

    sheet.setFrozenRows(1);
    return newSS.getUrl();
  } catch (e) { 
    throw new Error("Lỗi xuất Sheet: " + e.message); 
  }
}

function updateTransferStatus_PL(contractNumber, isTransferred, docTypes) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(20000); 
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    const logSheet = ss.getSheetByName("CHUYEN HO SO");
    const sheetLog = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
    if (!sheetLog || !logSheet) throw new Error("Hệ thống thiếu Sổ gốc hoặc CHUYEN HO SO");

    const lastRowLog = sheetLog.getLastRow();
    const dataE = sheetLog.getRange(1, 5, lastRowLog, 1).getValues(); // Cột E (Mã HĐ)
    const targetMa = contractNumber.trim().toUpperCase();
    let targetRow = -1;
    let ngayKyStr = "";

    for (let i = 0; i < dataE.length; i++) {
      if (dataE[i][0].toString().trim().toUpperCase() === targetMa) {
        targetRow = i + 1;
        let rawDate = sheetLog.getRange(targetRow, 2).getValue(); // Lấy ngày ký ở cột B
        if (rawDate instanceof Date) ngayKyStr = Utilities.formatDate(rawDate, "GMT+7", "dd/MM/yyyy");
        else ngayKyStr = rawDate ? rawDate.toString() : "";
        break;
      }
    }

    if (targetRow === -1) return false;

    // SỬA TẠI ĐÂY: Ghi dấu "x" bàn giao vào trực tiếp Cột O (Cột 15) trên Sổ Gốc
    sheetLog.getRange(targetRow, 15).setValue(isTransferred ? "x" : "");

    const lastRowLogChuyen = logSheet.getLastRow();
    
    if (isTransferred) {
      const senderName = getCurrentStaffName();
      const now = new Date();
      if (now.getHours() >= 17) now.setDate(now.getDate() + 1);
      if (now.getDay() === 6) now.setDate(now.getDate() + 2); 
      else if (now.getDay() === 0) now.setDate(now.getDate() + 1); 
      
      const timeString = Utilities.formatDate(now, "GMT+7", "dd/MM/yyyy");

      let loaiHoSo = "HỢP ĐỒNG";
      let soHopDongGoc = targetMa;
      let maDuAn = "";

      if (soHopDongGoc.match(/^PL\d{2}\s*-/)) {
        const parts = soHopDongGoc.split(" - ");
        loaiHoSo = parts[0].trim();
        soHopDongGoc = parts.slice(1).join(" - ").trim();
      }
      const projectMatch = soHopDongGoc.match(/-(.*?)\//);
      if (projectMatch && projectMatch[1]) maDuAn = projectMatch[1].trim();

      const newRowData = [["", maDuAn, loaiHoSo, soHopDongGoc, ngayKyStr, senderName, timeString, "", docTypes]];
      
      const nextRow = getNextRowAfterLastData_SO(logSheet, 4, 2, 4);
      logSheet.getRange(nextRow, 1, 1, 9).setValues(newRowData);

    } else {
      // Xóa trực tiếp hàng vật lý: An toàn tuyệt đối 100% kể cả khi Sheet đang bị Filter/Sort
      if (lastRowLogChuyen >= 4) {
        const dataRange = logSheet.getRange(4, 1, lastRowLogChuyen - 3, 4).getValues();
        
        let contractToFind = targetMa;
        if (contractToFind.match(/^PL\d{2}\s*-/)) {
          contractToFind = contractToFind.split(" - ").slice(1).join(" - ").trim();
        }

        // Quét từ dưới lên để tìm đúng dòng cần hủy và xóa hàng vật lý
        for (let i = dataRange.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          const loaiC = dataRange[i][2]?.toString().trim() || "";
          const valD = dataRange[i][3]?.toString().trim() || "";
          
          const isMatchMa = (valD === contractToFind);
          const isMatchLoai = targetMa.match(/^PL\d{2}\s*-/) ? loaiC.startsWith("PL") : loaiC === "HỢP ĐỒNG";
          
          if (isMatchMa && isMatchLoai) {
            logSheet.deleteRow(i + 4); // Xóa chính xác hàng, Google Sheets tự động cập nhật bộ lọc
            break; 
          }
        }
      }
    }

    return true;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock(); 
  }
}

// ==========================================
// --- NHÓM HÀM HỖ TRỢ PHỤ LỤC (HELPERS) ---
// ==========================================

function generateFileName_PL(field8) {
  if (!field8) return "BDH_PLXX_XXX_TEN GOI THAU_XXX";
  const parts = field8.split(" - ");
  const annexNumber = parts[0]?.trim() || "PLXX"; 
  const contractPart = parts[1]?.trim() || "160/2025/HĐTCXD-BDH/BCONS-TĐT"; 
  const contractNumber = (contractPart.match(/^([^/]+)/) || [])[1] || "XXX";
  const contractType = (contractPart.match(/-(.*?)\//) || [])[1] || "BDH";
  const lastPart = (contractPart.match(/-([^/]+)$/) || [])[1] || "XXX";
  return `${contractType}_${annexNumber}_${contractNumber}_TEN GOI THAU_${lastPart}`;
}

function computeAllActiveIds(rawIdsString) {
  if (!rawIdsString) return [];
  
  const selected = rawIdsString.toString().split(",").map(id => id.trim());
  const set = new Set(selected);
  let finalIds = [...selected];

  const hasA = set.has("1") || set.has("2");
  const hasB = set.has("3") || set.has("4") || set.has("5"); 

  if (hasA && hasB) finalIds.push("200");
  else if (hasA) finalIds.push("201");
  else if (hasB) finalIds.push("202");

  if (hasA) finalIds.push("300");
  if (hasB) finalIds.push("301");

  return finalIds;
}

// ==========================================
// --- PHÂN HỆ: QUẢN LÝ FILE & SCAN UTILITIES ---
// ==========================================

const ROOT_FOLDER_ID = "1SmlwbdIfTTQAaGTl2sEtRU-KFEYmx9Xc";

function uploadScanToDrive(base64Data, maHD, fileName) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(20000); 
    let projectName = "DEFAULT";
    const match = maHD.match(/HĐTCXD-([^\/\s]+)/i); 
    if (match) {
        projectName = match[1].trim().toUpperCase();
    }

    const rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(ROOT_FOLDER_ID);
    const folderIterator = rootFolder.getFoldersByName(projectName);
    
    let targetFolder;
    if (folderIterator.hasNext()) {
        targetFolder = folderIterator.next();
    } else {
        targetFolder = rootFolder.createFolder(projectName);
    }

    let mimeType = 'application/pdf'; 
    const lowerName = fileName.toLowerCase();
    if (lowerName.endsWith('.xlsx')) {
        mimeType = 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet';
    } else if (lowerName.endsWith('.xls')) {
        mimeType = 'application/vnd.ms-excel';
    }
    
    const blob = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(base64Data), mimeType, fileName);
    const file = targetFolder.createFile(blob);
    
    // Bảo vệ file gốc: chỉ cho phép xem (VIEW), không cho phép chỉnh sửa/xóa trực tiếp trên Drive
    try {
        file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    } catch(shareErr) {
        console.warn("Bỏ qua cảnh báo setSharing trên file Scan: " + shareErr.message);
    }
    
    const fileId = file.getId();

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    const sheetLog = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
    const lastRowLog = sheetLog.getLastRow();
    const dataE = sheetLog.getRange(1, 5, lastRowLog, 1).getValues().flat(); // Cột E (Mã HĐ)
    const targetIndex = dataE.findIndex(val => val.toString().trim().toUpperCase() === maHD.trim().toUpperCase());

    if (targetIndex !== -1) {
      const cell = sheetLog.getRange(targetIndex + 1, 16);
      SpreadsheetApp.flush(); 
      const currentVal = cell.getValue().toString();
      const newEntry = fileId + "|" + fileName;
      const newVal = (currentVal && currentVal.trim() !== "") ? (currentVal + ";;" + newEntry) : newEntry;
      cell.setValue(newVal);
      SpreadsheetApp.flush(); 
    }
    
    return { success: true, id: fileId, fileName: fileName };
  } catch (e) { 
    return { success: false, error: e.toString() }; 
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock(); 
  }
}

function getFileFromDrive(fileId) {
  try {
    const file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
    const blob = file.getBlob();
    const bytes = blob.getBytes();
    return Utilities.base64Encode(bytes);
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("Không thể truy cập file trên Drive: " + e.toString());
  }
}

function deleteContractRow_Backend(maHD) {
  var lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(20000); 
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    const maClean = maHD.toString().trim().toUpperCase();
    let fileIds = [];

    // 1. XỬ LÝ TRÊN SỔ GỐC (Tìm mã tại cột E để lấy file ID và xóa hàng)
    const sheetLog = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
    if (sheetLog) {
      const lastRowLog = sheetLog.getLastRow();
      const dataE = sheetLog.getRange(1, 5, lastRowLog, 1).getValues();
      const dataP = sheetLog.getRange(1, 16, lastRowLog, 1).getValues(); // Cột P (Cột 16) chứa File Scan
      
      for (let j = dataE.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
        if (dataE[j][0].toString().trim().toUpperCase() === maClean && maClean !== "") {
          const scanData = dataP[j][0].toString();
          if (scanData.includes("|")) {
            scanData.split(";;").forEach(e => fileIds.push(e.split("|")[0]));
          }
          // Xóa hàng sổ gốc
          sheetLog.deleteRow(j + 1);
          break; 
        }
      }
    }

    // 2. DỌN DRIVE
    if (fileIds.length > 0) {
      fileIds.forEach(id => { try { DriveApp.getFileById(id).setTrashed(true); } catch(e) {} });
    }

    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    return true;
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("Lỗi Backend xóa Cột E: " + e.message);
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock(); 
  }
}

function deleteScanFilePermanently(maHD, fileIdToDelete) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(20000); 
    if (!fileIdToDelete || !maHD) throw new Error("Thông tin yêu cầu không hợp lệ.");

    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    const sheetLog = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
    if (!sheetLog) throw new Error("Không tìm thấy Sổ gốc!");

    const lastRowLog = sheetLog.getLastRow();
    const dataE = sheetLog.getRange(1, 5, lastRowLog, 1).getValues().flat();
    const targetIndex = dataE.findIndex(val => val.toString().trim().toUpperCase() === maHD.trim().toUpperCase());

    if (targetIndex === -1) {
      throw new Error("Không tìm thấy hồ sơ hợp đồng tương ứng.");
    }

    // CHỐNG IDOR: Kiểm tra fileIdToDelete có thực sự nằm trong danh sách file scan của mã HĐ này không
    const cell = sheetLog.getRange(targetIndex + 1, 16);
    const currentVal = cell.getValue().toString();
    const isFileBelongsToContract = currentVal.includes(fileIdToDelete + "|");

    if (!isFileBelongsToContract) {
      throw new Error("BẢO MẬT: File không thuộc hồ sơ hợp đồng này, từ chối xóa!");
    }

    // Sau khi xác thực hợp lệ mới thực hiện xóa vật lý trên Drive
    try {
      Drive.Files.remove(fileIdToDelete);
    } catch (driveErr) {
      console.warn("File đã bị xóa trước đó trên Drive: " + driveErr.message);
    }

    const updatedFiles = currentVal.split(";;").filter(f => f && !f.startsWith(fileIdToDelete + "|"));
    if (updatedFiles.length > 0) {
      cell.setValue(updatedFiles.join(";;"));
    } else {
      cell.clearContent();
    }

    SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    return { success: true };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: false, error: e.toString() };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock(); 
  }
}

function getOrCreateSubFolder(parentFolder, folderName) {
  const folders = parentFolder.getFoldersByName(folderName);
  if (folders.hasNext()) return folders.next();
  return parentFolder.createFolder(folderName);
}

function optimizeDocSections(body, validSections) {
  const paragraphs = body.getParagraphs();
  let isDeleting = false;
  let currentBlock = [];
  let elementsToRemove = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < paragraphs.length; i++) {
    let p = paragraphs[i];
    let text = p.getText().trim();

    if (!isDeleting && text.includes("{")) {
      isDeleting = true;
      currentBlock = [p];
    } 
    else if (isDeleting) {
      currentBlock.push(p);
    }

    if (isDeleting && text.includes("}")) {
      let match = text.match(/\}\s*(\d+)/);
      if (match && match[1]) {
        if (!validSections.has(match[1].toString())) {
          elementsToRemove.push(...currentBlock);
        }
      }
      isDeleting = false;
      currentBlock = [];
    }
  }

  elementsToRemove.forEach(el => {
    try { el.removeFromParent(); } catch(e) {}
  });

  body.replaceText("\\{", "");
  body.replaceText("\\}\\s*\\d+", "");
}

function markScanDigitized_PL(fileId) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(20000);
    if (!fileId) return false;
    const targetId = fileId.toString().trim();
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
    const sheetLog = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
    if (!sheetLog) return false;

    const lastRow = sheetLog.getLastRow();
    if (lastRow < 3) return false;

    const valuesP = sheetLog.getRange(3, 16, lastRow - 2, 1).getValues();
    let updated = false;

    for (let i = 0; i < valuesP.length; i++) {
      const raw = (valuesP[i][0] || "").toString().trim();
      if (!raw || !raw.includes(targetId + "|")) continue;

      const entries = raw.split(";;").filter(Boolean);
      let rowChanged = false;
      const newEntries = entries.map(entry => {
        const parts = entry.split("|");
        if (parts[0].trim() === targetId && (parts[2] || "").trim() !== "1") {
          rowChanged = true;
          return `${parts[0].trim()}|${(parts[1] || "").trim()}|1`;
        }
        return entry;
      });

      if (rowChanged) {
        sheetLog.getRange(i + 3, 16).setValue(newEntries.join(";;"));
        updated = true;
      }
    }

    if (updated) SpreadsheetApp.flush();
    return updated;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Lỗi markScanDigitized_PL: " + e.message);
    return false;
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/**
 * HÀM CHẠY TAY 1 LẦN TRONG APPS SCRIPT:
 * Tự động quét các file đã có "[Số hóa] *.json" từ trước để chuyển chấm sang Xanh lá
 */
function syncExistingDigitizedFlags_PL() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);
  const sheetLog = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
  if (!sheetLog) return;

  const lastRow = sheetLog.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 3) return;

  const valuesP = sheetLog.getRange(3, 16, lastRow - 2, 1).getValues();
  for (let i = 0; i < valuesP.length; i++) {
    const raw = (valuesP[i][0] || "").toString().trim();
    if (!raw || !raw.includes("|")) continue;

    const entries = raw.split(";;").filter(Boolean);
    let rowChanged = false;

    const newEntries = entries.map(entry => {
      const parts = entry.split("|");
      const fId = parts[0].trim();
      const fName = (parts[1] || "").trim();
      if ((parts[2] || "").trim() === "1") return entry;

      try {
        const file = DriveApp.getFileById(fId);
        const parents = file.getParents();
        if (parents.hasNext()) {
          const folder = parents.next();
          const cleanBase = fName.replace(/\.pdf$/i, "").trim();
          const hasJson = folder.getFilesByName(`[Số hóa] ${cleanBase}.json`).hasNext() ||
                          folder.getFilesByName(`${cleanBase}.json`).hasNext();
          if (hasJson) {
            rowChanged = true;
            return `${fId}|${fName}|1`;
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {}
      return entry;
    });

    if (rowChanged) {
      sheetLog.getRange(i + 3, 16).setValue(newEntries.join(";;"));
    }
  }
  SpreadsheetApp.flush();
}

/**
 * Ghi đè nội dung nhị phân PDF sạch lên đúng fileId gốc trên Drive (giữ nguyên ID & quyền chia sẻ)
 */
function overwritePdfFile_Backend(fileId, base64Data) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(20000);
    if (!fileId || !base64Data) throw new Error("Thiếu fileId hoặc dữ liệu PDF.");

    const targetId = String(fileId).trim();
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.openById(SHEET_ID);

    // CHỐNG IDOR: Xác minh fileId có tồn tại trong hệ thống quản lý trước khi ghi đè
    let isPermitted = false;

    // 1. Kiểm tra trong Sổ Hợp Đồng (Cột P)
    const sheetLog = ss.getSheetByName("SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP");
    if (sheetLog && sheetLog.getLastRow() >= 3) {
      const scans = sheetLog.getRange(3, 16, sheetLog.getLastRow() - 2, 1).getValues().flat();
      if (scans.some(v => v && String(v).includes(targetId + "|"))) {
        isPermitted = true;
      }
    }

    // 2. Kiểm tra trong Drawing_Log (Cột B) nếu chưa thấy ở Sổ HĐ
    if (!isPermitted) {
      const drawSheet = ss.getSheetByName("Drawing_Log");
      if (drawSheet && drawSheet.getLastRow() >= 2) {
        const drawFiles = drawSheet.getRange(2, 2, drawSheet.getLastRow() - 1, 1).getValues().flat();
        if (drawFiles.some(v => v && String(v).trim() === targetId)) {
          isPermitted = true;
        }
      }
    }

    if (!isPermitted) {
      throw new Error("BẢO MẬT: Tệp tin không thuộc phạm vi hồ sơ được quản lý!");
    }

    const blob = Utilities.newBlob(Utilities.base64Decode(base64Data), "application/pdf");
    Drive.Files.update({}, targetId, blob, { supportsAllDrives: true });
    return { success: true };
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("Không thể ghi đè file PDF trên Drive: " + e.message);
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

/**
 * Khởi tạo phiên Resumable PATCH dùng 1 lần để Client ghi đè trực tiếp PDF sạch lên Drive
 * (Không lộ OAuth Token + Kiểm tra IDOR siêu tốc bằng Sheets REST API)
 */
function getPdfOverwriteSession_Backend(fileId, fileSize, clientOrigin) {
  try {
    if (!fileId || !fileSize) throw new Error("Thiếu fileId hoặc kích thước tệp.");
    const targetId = String(fileId).trim();

    // 1. CHỐNG IDOR SIÊU TỐC (~150ms) qua Google Sheets REST API v4
    const resSheets = Sheets.Spreadsheets.Values.batchGet(SHEET_ID, {
      ranges: ["SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP!P3:P", "Drawing_Log!B2:B"]
    });
    const ranges = resSheets.valueRanges || [];
    const scanRows = (ranges[0]?.values || []).flat();
    const drawRows = (ranges[1]?.values || []).flat();

    const isPermitted =
      scanRows.some(v => v && String(v).includes(targetId + "|")) ||
      drawRows.some(v => v && String(v).trim() === targetId);

    if (!isPermitted) {
      throw new Error("BẢO MẬT: Tệp tin không thuộc phạm vi hồ sơ được quản lý!");
    }

    // 2. Xin phiên Resumable PATCH chỉ áp dụng cho đúng targetId
    const apiURL = `https://www.googleapis.com/upload/drive/v3/files/${encodeURIComponent(targetId)}?uploadType=resumable&supportsAllDrives=true`;
    const headers = {
      Authorization: "Bearer " + ScriptApp.getOAuthToken(),
      "Content-Type": "application/json; charset=UTF-8",
      "X-Upload-Content-Type": "application/pdf",
      "X-Upload-Content-Length": String(fileSize)
    };
    if (clientOrigin) {
      headers["Origin"] = String(clientOrigin).trim();
    }

    const response = UrlFetchApp.fetch(apiURL, {
      method: "patch",
      headers: headers,
      payload: "{}",
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    });

    if (response.getResponseCode() !== 200) {
      throw new Error("Drive từ chối phiên ghi đè (" + response.getResponseCode() + "): " + response.getContentText());
    }

    const respHeaders = response.getHeaders();
    const uploadUrl = respHeaders["Location"] || respHeaders["location"];
    if (!uploadUrl) throw new Error("Không nhận được đường dẫn Resumable PATCH.");

    return { success: true, uploadUrl: uploadUrl };
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("Lỗi khởi tạo phiên ghi đè PDF: " + e.message);
  }
}


=== FILE: backend/API_Viewer.js ===
// =========================================================================
// MODULE VIEWER: CLOUD VISION AI & FAST DRIVE CACHING
// =========================================================================

const VIEWER_CHUNK_SIZE = 2500000;
const VIEWER_RAW_PER_CHUNK = (VIEWER_CHUNK_SIZE / 4) * 3;

function getJsonCacheName_Viewer_(fileName) {
  const cleanBase = (fileName || "").normalize("NFC").replace(/\.pdf$/i, "").trim();
  return "[Số hóa] " + (cleanBase || "TaiLieu") + ".json";
}

function getFolderForFile_Viewer_(fileId) {
  if (!fileId || String(fileId).indexOf("local_") === 0) {
    return DriveApp.getRootFolder();
  }
  const file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
  const parents = file.getParents();
  if (parents.hasNext()) return parents.next();
  throw new Error("Không tìm thấy thư mục chứa tệp trên Drive.");
}

function findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, name) {
  if (!name || !folder) return null;
  const files = folder.getFilesByName(name);
  while (files.hasNext()) {
    const f = files.next();
    if (!f.isTrashed()) return f;
  }
  return null;
}

function getDriveCacheFast(fileId, fileName) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  let locked = false;
  try {
    locked = lock.tryLock(1500);
  } catch (e) {
    locked = false;
  }
  if (!locked) return { found: false, isOptimizing: false, busy: true };

  try {
    const scriptCache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
    const folder = getFolderForFile_Viewer_(fileId);
    const targetJsonName = getJsonCacheName_Viewer_(fileName);
    const cleanBase = (fileName || "").normalize("NFC").replace(/\.pdf$/i, "").trim();

    let targetFile = findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, targetJsonName);
    if (!targetFile) {
      targetFile = findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, targetJsonName.normalize("NFD"));
    }
    if (!targetFile && cleanBase) {
      targetFile = findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, cleanBase + ".json") ||
                   findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, (cleanBase + ".json").normalize("NFD"));
    }

    if (targetFile) {
      scriptCache.remove("LOCK_OPT_" + fileId);
      return { found: true, isOptimizing: false, data: targetFile.getBlob().getDataAsString() };
    }

    if (scriptCache.get("LOCK_OPT_" + fileId)) {
      return { found: false, isOptimizing: true };
    }

    scriptCache.put("LOCK_OPT_" + fileId, String(Date.now()), 600);
    return { found: false, isOptimizing: false };
  } catch (e) {
    // Ném lỗi thật để Client retry thay vì hiểu nhầm là "chưa số hóa" rồi đốt Vision API
    throw new Error("Lỗi đọc cache Drive: " + e.message);
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function saveDriveCacheFast(fileId, fileName, jsonDataString) {
  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  try {
    lock.waitLock(10000);
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("Hệ thống bận ghi dữ liệu, vui lòng thử lại.");
  }

  const scriptCache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  try {
    const folder = getFolderForFile_Viewer_(fileId);
    const targetJsonName = getJsonCacheName_Viewer_(fileName);
    let targetFile = findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, targetJsonName);

    if (targetFile) {
      targetFile.setContent(jsonDataString);
    } else {
      targetFile = folder.createFile(targetJsonName, jsonDataString, MimeType.PLAIN_TEXT);
    }

    const cleanBase = (fileName || "").replace(/\.pdf$/i, "").trim();
    if (cleanBase) {
      const oldFiles = folder.getFilesByName(cleanBase + ".json");
      while (oldFiles.hasNext()) {
        const oldF = oldFiles.next();
        if (!oldF.isTrashed() && oldF.getId() !== targetFile.getId()) oldF.setTrashed(true);
      }
    }

    if (!isPlOrPdgFile_Viewer_(fileName)) {
      try {
        updatePl01IndexInternal_(fileId, jsonDataString, null);
      } catch (idxErr) {
        console.warn("Bỏ qua lỗi cập nhật Index_PL01:", idxErr.message);
      }
    }

    scriptCache.remove("LOCK_OPT_" + fileId);
    return { success: true, jsonId: targetFile.getId() };
  } catch (e) {
    scriptCache.remove("LOCK_OPT_" + fileId);
    throw new Error("Lỗi lưu cache: " + e.message);
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}

function getServiceAccountReadOnlyToken_() {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const cachedToken = cache.get("SA_DRIVE_RO_TOKEN");
  if (cachedToken) return cachedToken;

  const props = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties();
  const clientEmail = (props.getProperty("SA_CLIENT_EMAIL") || "").trim();
  const rawPrivateKey = props.getProperty("SA_PRIVATE_KEY") || "";
  const privateKey = rawPrivateKey.replace(/\\n/g, "\n").trim();

  if (!clientEmail || !privateKey) {
    throw new Error("Chưa cấu hình SA_CLIENT_EMAIL hoặc SA_PRIVATE_KEY trong Script Properties!");
  }

  const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
  const header = { alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" };
  const claimSet = {
    iss: clientEmail,
    scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/drive.readonly",
    aud: "https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token",
    exp: now + 3600,
    iat: now
  };

  const toB64Url = (input) => Utilities.base64EncodeWebSafe(input).replace(/=+$/, "");
  const signingInput = toB64Url(JSON.stringify(header)) + "." + toB64Url(JSON.stringify(claimSet));
  const signatureBytes = Utilities.computeRsaSha256Signature(signingInput, privateKey);
  const jwt = signingInput + "." + toB64Url(signatureBytes);

  const res = UrlFetchApp.fetch("https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token", {
    method: "post",
    contentType: "application/x-www-form-urlencoded",
    payload: "grant_type=urn%3Aietf%3Aparams%3Aoauth%3Agrant-type%3Ajwt-bearer&assertion=" + encodeURIComponent(jwt),
    muteHttpExceptions: true
  });

  const code = res.getResponseCode();
  const data = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
  if (code !== 200 || !data.access_token) {
    throw new Error("Không thể cấp Service Account Token: " + (data.error_description || data.error || code));
  }

  cache.put("SA_DRIVE_RO_TOKEN", data.access_token, 3000);
  return data.access_token;
}

function getDriveAccessToken() {
  return getServiceAccountReadOnlyToken_();
}

function makeFilePublicAndGetLink(fileId) {
  try {
    if (!fileId || String(fileId).indexOf("local_") === 0) {
      return { success: false, error: "File cục bộ chưa có trên Drive" };
    }
    const file = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId);
    file.setSharing(DriveApp.Access.ANYONE_WITH_LINK, DriveApp.Permission.VIEW);
    return {
      success: true,
      url: "https://drive.google.com/file/d/" + fileId + "/preview"
    };
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: false, error: e.message };
  }
}

function callCloudVisionApiBatch(imagesBase64Array) {
  try {
    enforceAiGuard_("VISION", imagesBase64Array, 16, 20);
    const rawKey = PropertiesService.getScriptProperties().getProperty("VISION_API_KEY");
    if (!rawKey) throw new Error("Chưa cài đặt khóa 'VISION_API_KEY' trong Script Properties!");

    // Tự động làm sạch khoảng trắng thừa
    const apiKey = rawKey.trim();

    const url = "https://vision.googleapis.com/v1/images:annotate?key=" + apiKey;
    const MAX_VISION_BATCH = 16;
    let allResponses = [];

    for (let i = 0; i < imagesBase64Array.length; i += MAX_VISION_BATCH) {
      const slice = imagesBase64Array.slice(i, i + MAX_VISION_BATCH);
      const requests = slice.map(function(b64) {
        return {
          image: { content: String(b64).replace(/^data:image\/\w+;base64,/, "") },
          features: [{ type: "DOCUMENT_TEXT_DETECTION" }],
          imageContext: { languageHints: ["vi", "en"] }
        };
      });

      const res = UrlFetchApp.fetch(url, {
        method: "post",
        contentType: "application/json",
        payload: JSON.stringify({ requests: requests }),
        muteHttpExceptions: true
      });

      const resJson = JSON.parse(res.getContentText());
      if (resJson.error) {
        const errorDetail = resJson.error.details ? JSON.stringify(resJson.error.details) : "";
        throw new Error(resJson.error.message + (errorDetail ? " -> Details: " + errorDetail : ""));
      }
      if (resJson.responses) {
        allResponses = allResponses.concat(resJson.responses);
      }
    }

    return allResponses.map(function(resp) {
      if (!resp || !resp.fullTextAnnotation || !resp.fullTextAnnotation.pages) {
        return { words: [], rotation: 0 };
      }

      const pObj = resp.fullTextAnnotation.pages[0];
      const imgW = pObj.width || 0;
      const imgH = pObj.height || 0;

      let angle0 = 0, angle90 = 0, angle180 = 0, angle270 = 0;
      const rawWords = [];

      pObj.blocks.forEach(function(b) {
        b.paragraphs.forEach(function(p) {
          p.words.forEach(function(w) {
            const str = w.symbols.map(function(s) { return s.text; }).join("");
            const v = w.boundingBox.vertices || [];
            if (v.length >= 2 && v[0] && v[1]) {
              const dx = (v[1].x || 0) - (v[0].x || 0);
              const dy = (v[1].y || 0) - (v[0].y || 0);
              if (Math.abs(dx) >= Math.abs(dy)) {
                if (dx >= 0) angle0++;
                else angle180++;
              } else {
                if (dy > 0) angle90++;
                else angle270++;
              }
            }

            let minX = null, minY = null, maxX = null, maxY = null;
            for (let i = 0; i < v.length; i++) {
              const vx = v[i].x || 0;
              const vy = v[i].y || 0;
              if (minX === null || vx < minX) minX = vx;
              if (minY === null || vy < minY) minY = vy;
              if (maxX === null || vx > maxX) maxX = vx;
              if (maxY === null || vy > maxY) maxY = vy;
            }

            rawWords.push({
              str: str,
              x: minX || 0,
              y: minY || 0,
              w: Math.max(1, (maxX || 0) - (minX || 0)),
              h: Math.max(1, (maxY || 0) - (minY || 0))
            });
          });
        });
      });

      let detectedAngle = 0;
      const maxVotes = Math.max(angle0, angle90, angle180, angle270);
      if (maxVotes > 0) {
        if (maxVotes === angle90) detectedAngle = 90;
        else if (maxVotes === angle270) detectedAngle = 270;
        else if (maxVotes === angle180) detectedAngle = 180;
      }

      let pdfRotation = 0;
      if (detectedAngle === 90) pdfRotation = 270;
      else if (detectedAngle === 270) pdfRotation = 90;
      else if (detectedAngle === 180) pdfRotation = 180;

      const correctedWords = rawWords.map(function(w) {
        let nx = w.x, ny = w.y, nw = w.w, nh = w.h;

        if (pdfRotation === 270) {
          nx = w.y;
          ny = Math.max(0, imgW - (w.x + w.w));
          nw = w.h;
          nh = w.w;
        } else if (pdfRotation === 90) {
          nx = Math.max(0, imgH - (w.y + w.h));
          ny = w.x;
          nw = w.h;
          nh = w.w;
        } else if (pdfRotation === 180) {
          nx = Math.max(0, imgW - (w.x + w.w));
          ny = Math.max(0, imgH - (w.y + w.h));
        }

        return { str: w.str, x: nx, y: ny, w: nw, h: nh };
      });

      return { words: correctedWords, rotation: pdfRotation };
    });
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error("Lỗi Cloud Vision Batch: " + e.message);
  }
}

function getPdfBase64Fast(fileId) {
  return Utilities.base64Encode(DriveApp.getFileById(fileId).getBlob().getBytes());
}

function fetchDriveByteRange_Viewer_(fileId, start, endInclusive) {
  const token = ScriptApp.getOAuthToken();
  const res = UrlFetchApp.fetch(
    "https://www.googleapis.com/drive/v3/files/" + encodeURIComponent(fileId) + "?alt=media&supportsAllDrives=true",
    {
      method: "get",
      headers: {
        Authorization: "Bearer " + token,
        Range: "bytes=" + start + "-" + endInclusive
      },
      muteHttpExceptions: true
    }
  );
  const code = res.getResponseCode();
  if (code !== 200 && code !== 206) throw new Error("Drive range HTTP " + code);
  return { code: code, bytes: res.getContent() };
}

function getPdfBase64Chunk(fileId, chunkIndex) {
  const size = DriveApp.getFileById(fileId).getSize();
  const b64Len = Math.ceil(size / 3) * 4;
  const totalChunks = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(b64Len / VIEWER_CHUNK_SIZE));
  const idx = Math.max(0, Number(chunkIndex) || 0);
  const rawStart = idx * VIEWER_RAW_PER_CHUNK;
  if (!size || rawStart >= size) return { totalChunks: totalChunks, chunk: "" };

  const rawEnd = Math.min(size, rawStart + VIEWER_RAW_PER_CHUNK) - 1;
  const ranged = fetchDriveByteRange_Viewer_(fileId, rawStart, rawEnd);
  let bytes = ranged.bytes;
  const expect = rawEnd - rawStart + 1;
  if (ranged.code === 200 && bytes.length > expect) {
    bytes = bytes.slice(rawStart, rawEnd + 1);
  }
  return {
    totalChunks: totalChunks,
    chunk: Utilities.base64Encode(bytes).replace(/\s+/g, "")
  };
}

// =========================================================================
// HẠ TẦNG INDEX SHARDING THEO NĂM (Index_PL01_<YEAR>.json) & BACKFILL
// =========================================================================

function getIndexRootFolderId_Viewer_() {
  return (typeof ROOT_FOLDER_ID !== "undefined" && ROOT_FOLDER_ID)
    ? ROOT_FOLDER_ID
    : "1SmlwbdIfTTQAaGTl2sEtRU-KFEYmx9Xc";
}

function getIndexFileName_Viewer_(year) {
  return "Index_PL01_" + String(year).trim() + ".json";
}

function isPlOrPdgFile_Viewer_(fileName) {
  if (!fileName) return false;
  const clean = String(fileName).toLowerCase();
  const isPdg = /pdg|pđg|phe\s*duyet\s*gia|phê\s*duyệt\s*giá|(\b|_)gia(\b|_)|(\b|_)giá(\b|_)/i.test(clean);
  const isPl = /(^|[^a-z0-9])pl(\d+)?([^a-z0-9]|$)|phu\s*luc|phụ\s*lục/i.test(clean);
  return isPdg || isPl;
}

function removeDiacriticsLower_Viewer_(str) {
  if (!str) return "";
  return String(str)
    .normalize("NFD")
    .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
    .replace(/đ/g, "d")
    .replace(/Đ/g, "D")
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

function compressGzipString_Viewer_(rawStr) {
  const blob = Utilities.newBlob(String(rawStr || ""), "application/json", "index.json");
  const gzBytes = Utilities.gzip(blob).getBytes();
  return "GZ:" + Utilities.base64Encode(gzBytes);
}

function decompressGzipString_Viewer_(payloadStr) {
  if (!payloadStr || typeof payloadStr !== "string") return "";
  const trimmed = payloadStr.trim();
  if (trimmed.startsWith("{") || trimmed.startsWith("[")) return trimmed;
  if (trimmed.startsWith("GZ:")) {
    const rawBytes = Utilities.base64Decode(trimmed.slice(3));
    const gzBlob = Utilities.newBlob(rawBytes, "application/x-gzip");
    return Utilities.ungzip(gzBlob).getDataAsString("UTF-8");
  }
  return trimmed;
}

function isStandaloneNumberOrMoney_Viewer_(tok) {
  if (!tok) return false;
  const s = tok.trim();
  if (!s) return false;
  if (/^[-–—]+$/.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^[₫đ%]+$/i.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^\(?-?\d{1,3}(?:[.,]\d{3})+(?:[.,]\d+)?%?\)?$/.test(s)) return true;
  if (/^-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?%?$/.test(s)) return true;
  return false;
}

function cleanPl01Punctuation_Viewer_(str) {
  if (!str) return "";
  const PH_DOT = "\uE001";
  const PH_COMMA = "\uE002";
  let s = String(str)
    .replace(/(\d)\.(?=\d)/g, "$1" + PH_DOT)
    .replace(/(\d),(?=\d)/g, "$1" + PH_COMMA);

  s = s.replace(/[^a-zA-ZÀ-ỹ0-9\uE001\uE002]+/g, " ");

  return s
    .replace(/\uE001/g, ".")
    .replace(/\uE002/g, ",")
    .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
    .trim();
}

function extractCleanPl01PagesFromManifest_(manifest) {
  if (!manifest || !Array.isArray(manifest.pages) || manifest.pages.length === 0) return [];
  const total = manifest.numPages || manifest.pages.length;
  const apStart = Number(manifest.appendixStartPage);
  if (!apStart || apStart > total) return [];

  const INLINE_UNIT_NEXT_RE = /^(canh|lop|lot|phu|tang|phut|gio|ngay|thang|nam|bo|chiec|cai|mat|ben|loai|phan|dot|mm|cm|m|m2|m3|md|kg|tan|lit|ong|vien|tam|thanh|cay|soi|cuon|thung|bao|cap|doi|he|bac|buoc)$/;
  const PREV_DESCRIPTOR_RE = /^(tang|loai|so|dot|phan|khu|block|thap|can|truc|mau|cap|he|bac|buoc|lo|muc|dieu|khoan|pl|nam|thang|ngay|gio|phut|lan|cot|day|nhom|phong|canh|lop|lot|phu)$/;

  const resultPages = [];

  for (let pIdx = 0; pIdx < manifest.pages.length; pIdx++) {
    const p = manifest.pages[pIdx];
    const pNum = Number(p.pageNum) || (pIdx + 1);
    if (pNum < apStart || !Array.isArray(p.textItems) || p.textItems.length === 0) continue;

    const pageW = Number(p.width) || 595;
    const pageH = Number(p.height) || 842;

    const normItems = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < p.textItems.length; i++) {
      const it = p.textItems[i];
      const str = Array.isArray(it) ? String(it[0] || "").trim() : String(it.str || "").trim();
      if (!str) continue;
      const x = Number(Array.isArray(it) ? it[1] : it.x) || 0;
      const y = Number(Array.isArray(it) ? it[2] : it.y) || 0;
      const w = Number(Array.isArray(it) ? it[3] : it.w) || 0;
      const h = Number(Array.isArray(it) ? it[4] : it.h) || 10;
      const topY = pageH - (y + h);
      normItems.push({ str, x, w, h, topY });
    }

    if (normItems.length === 0) continue;
    normItems.sort((a, b) => (a.topY - b.topY) || (a.x - b.x));

    const lines = [];
    let curLine = [normItems[0]];
    let anchorTopY = normItems[0].topY;
    let anchorH = normItems[0].h || 10;

    for (let i = 1; i < normItems.length; i++) {
      const item = normItems[i];
      const tol = Math.max(4, Math.min(anchorH, item.h || 10) * 0.55);
      if (Math.abs(item.topY - anchorTopY) <= tol) {
        curLine.push(item);
      } else {
        lines.push(curLine);
        curLine = [item];
        anchorTopY = item.topY;
        anchorH = item.h || 10;
      }
    }
    lines.push(curLine);

    const preparedRows = [];
    const dvtCandidatesX = [];

    for (let l = 0; l < lines.length; l++) {
      const row = lines[l];
      row.sort((a, b) => a.x - b.x);

      while (row.length > 0 && /^[^a-zA-ZÀ-ỹ0-9]+$/.test(row[0].str.trim())) {
        row.shift();
      }
      if (row.length === 0) continue;

      let hasStt = false;
      let sttStripped = 0;
      while (row.length > 1 && sttStripped < 2) {
        const t0 = row[0];
        const t1 = row[1];
        const s0 = t0.str.trim();
        const isSttPattern = /^(?:\d{1,4}(?:\.\d{1,2}){0,2}|[IVX]{1,4}|[a-z])[.)]?$/i.test(s0) || /^[.)]$/.test(s0);
        if (!isSttPattern) break;

        const gap0 = t1.x - (t0.x + t0.w);
        const avgH0 = Math.max(6, (t0.h + t1.h) / 2);
        const t1Norm = removeDiacriticsLower_Viewer_(t1.str);

        if (/^\d{1,4}$/.test(s0) && gap0 <= avgH0 * 0.75 && INLINE_UNIT_NEXT_RE.test(t1Norm)) {
          break;
        }
        if (t0.x < pageW * 0.16 || /[.)]$/.test(s0) || gap0 > Math.max(6, avgH0 * 0.85)) {
          row.shift();
          sttStripped++;
          hasStt = true;
        } else {
          break;
        }
      }

      let poppedNumericCount = 0;
      while (row.length > 1) {
        const last = row[row.length - 1];
        const prev = row[row.length - 2];
        const sLast = last.str.trim();

        if (/^[^a-zA-ZÀ-ỹ0-9]+$/.test(sLast) && !/^[-–—]+$/.test(sLast)) {
          row.pop();
          continue;
        }
        if (!isStandaloneNumberOrMoney_Viewer_(sLast)) break;

        const hasMoneySep = /[.,]\d/.test(sLast) || /^[-–—]+$/.test(sLast) || /^[₫đ%]+$/i.test(sLast);
        const gap = last.x - (prev.x + prev.w);
        const avgH = Math.max(6, (prev.h + last.h) / 2);
        const prevNorm = removeDiacriticsLower_Viewer_(prev.str);

        if (!hasMoneySep && PREV_DESCRIPTOR_RE.test(prevNorm) && gap <= Math.max(8, avgH * 0.95)) {
          break;
        }
        if (hasMoneySep || gap > Math.max(8, avgH * 1.1) || last.x > pageW * 0.6) {
          row.pop();
          poppedNumericCount++;
        } else {
          break;
        }
      }

      if (poppedNumericCount > 0 && row.length > 1) {
        const last = row[row.length - 1];
        const prev = row[row.length - 2];
        const avgH = Math.max(6, (prev.h + last.h) / 2);
        const gap = last.x - (prev.x + prev.w);
        const lastLen = last.str.trim().length;
        if (gap > Math.max(8, avgH * 0.9) && last.x > pageW * 0.35 && lastLen <= 14) {
          dvtCandidatesX.push({ x: last.x, xEnd: last.x + last.w });
          row.pop();
        }
      }

      if (row.length === 0) continue;
      if (row.length === 1 && isStandaloneNumberOrMoney_Viewer_(row[0].str)) continue;

      const topY = row[0].topY;
      const avgH = row.reduce((sum, it) => sum + it.h, 0) / row.length;
      preparedRows.push({ items: row, hasStt: hasStt, topY: topY, avgH: avgH });
    }

    let dvtRange = null;
    if (dvtCandidatesX.length >= 2) {
      const xs = dvtCandidatesX.map(c => c.x).sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const xEnds = dvtCandidatesX.map(c => c.xEnd).sort((a, b) => a - b);
      const medX = xs[Math.floor(xs.length / 2)];
      const medEnd = xEnds[Math.floor(xEnds.length / 2)];
      dvtRange = { minX: medX - 24, maxX: medEnd + 24 };
    }

    // GOM CÁC DÒNG CÙNG 1 Ô THÀNH ITEM BLOCK (Tối đa 3 dòng con)
    const itemBlocks = [];
    let curBlockItems = [];
    let curBlockLineCount = 0;
    let prevRowTopY = -999;
    let prevRowAvgH = 10;

    for (let rIdx = 0; rIdx < preparedRows.length; rIdx++) {
      const rObj = preparedRows[rIdx];
      const row = rObj.items;

      if (dvtRange && row.length > 1) {
        while (row.length > 1) {
          const last = row[row.length - 1];
          const prev = row[row.length - 2];
          const avgH = Math.max(6, (prev.h + last.h) / 2);
          const gap = last.x - (prev.x + prev.w);
          const inDvtCol = last.x >= dvtRange.minX && (last.x + last.w) <= dvtRange.maxX;
          if (inDvtCol && gap > Math.max(10, avgH * 1.1) && last.str.trim().length <= 14) {
            row.pop();
          } else {
            break;
          }
        }
      }
      if (row.length === 0) continue;

      const yGap = rObj.topY - prevRowTopY;
      const isContinuation = !rObj.hasStt &&
        curBlockLineCount > 0 &&
        curBlockLineCount < 3 &&
        yGap > 0 &&
        yGap <= Math.max(26, prevRowAvgH * 2.3);

      if (isContinuation) {
        curBlockItems.push(...row);
        curBlockLineCount++;
      } else {
        if (curBlockItems.length > 0) {
          itemBlocks.push(curBlockItems);
        }
        curBlockItems = [...row];
        curBlockLineCount = 1;
      }

      prevRowTopY = rObj.topY;
      prevRowAvgH = rObj.avgH;
    }
    if (curBlockItems.length > 0) {
      itemBlocks.push(curBlockItems);
    }

    const cleanBlocks = [];

    for (let bIdx = 0; bIdx < itemBlocks.length; bIdx++) {
      const blkItems = itemBlocks[bIdx];
      const joinedRaw = blkItems.map(t => t.str).join(" ");
      const blockStr = cleanPl01Punctuation_Viewer_(joinedRaw);
      if (!blockStr || blockStr.length < 2 || /^[^a-zA-ZÀ-ỹ0-9]+$/.test(blockStr)) continue;

      const normBlock = removeDiacriticsLower_Viewer_(blockStr);
      const isTableHeader = normBlock.includes("stt") &&
        (normBlock.includes("noi dung") || normBlock.includes("hang muc") || normBlock.includes("dien giai") || normBlock.includes("ten vat tu")) &&
        (normBlock.includes("don gia") || normBlock.includes("thanh tien") || normBlock.includes("khoi luong") || normBlock.includes("dvt"));
      const isAdminBoilerplate =
        /^cong hoa xa hoi chu nghia viet nam\b/.test(normBlock) ||
        /^doc lap tu do hanh phuc\b/.test(normBlock) ||
        (/^trang \d+( \d+)?$/.test(normBlock)) ||
        ((/^(dai dien ben a|dai dien ben b|ben a|ben b|tong giam doc|pho tong giam doc|giam doc)\b/.test(normBlock)) && normBlock.length <= 45) ||
        ((/^(ky ten|ky va ghi ro ho ten|dong dau)\b/.test(normBlock)) && normBlock.length <= 35);

      if (isTableHeader || isAdminBoilerplate) continue;
      cleanBlocks.push(blockStr);
    }

    if (cleanBlocks.length > 0) {
      resultPages.push([pNum, cleanBlocks]);
    }
  }

  return resultPages;
}

function extractYearFromContract_Viewer_(maHD, rawDate, fileId) {
  if (maHD) {
    const m1 = String(maHD).match(/\/(20\d{2})\//);
    if (m1) return m1[1];
  }
  if (rawDate) {
    const m2 = String(rawDate).match(/\b(20\d{2})\b/);
    if (m2) return m2[1];
  }
  if (fileId) {
    try {
      return String(DriveApp.getFileById(fileId).getDateCreated().getFullYear());
    } catch (e) {}
  }
  return String(new Date().getFullYear());
}

function resolveContractMetaForFileId_(fileId) {
  const res = Sheets.Spreadsheets.Values.get(SHEET_ID, "SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP!B3:P", {
    valueRenderOption: "FORMATTED_VALUE"
  });
  const rows = res.values || [];
  const target = String(fileId).trim();

  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const r = rows[i];
    const scanStr = String(r[14] || "");
    if (!scanStr || !scanStr.includes(target + "|")) continue;
    const maHD = String(r[3] || "").trim();
    if (/^PL\d+/i.test(maHD)) continue;
    const rawDate = String(r[0] || "").trim();
    return {
      maHD: maHD,
      year: extractYearFromContract_Viewer_(maHD, rawDate, target)
    };
  }
  return {
    maHD: "",
    year: extractYearFromContract_Viewer_("", "", target)
  };
}

function loadPl01IndexObj_(year) {
  const rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(getIndexRootFolderId_Viewer_());
  const fileName = getIndexFileName_Viewer_(year);
  const existing = findFileByName_Viewer_(rootFolder, fileName);

  if (!existing) {
    return {
      file: null,
      indexObj: { year: String(year), updatedAt: Date.now(), version: 1, files: {} }
    };
  }

  try {
    const raw = existing.getBlob().getDataAsString("UTF-8");
    const jsonStr = decompressGzipString_Viewer_(raw);
    const parsed = JSON.parse(jsonStr);
    if (!parsed.files || typeof parsed.files !== "object") parsed.files = {};
    parsed.year = String(year);
    return { file: existing, indexObj: parsed };
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("Lỗi đọc Index_PL01_" + year + ", khởi tạo mới:", e.message);
    return {
      file: existing,
      indexObj: { year: String(year), updatedAt: Date.now(), version: 1, files: {} }
    };
  }
}

function savePl01IndexObj_(year, indexObj, existingFile) {
  const rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(getIndexRootFolderId_Viewer_());
  const fileName = getIndexFileName_Viewer_(year);
  indexObj.year = String(year);
  indexObj.updatedAt = Date.now();
  indexObj.version = 1;

  const compressed = compressGzipString_Viewer_(JSON.stringify(indexObj));
  const target = existingFile || findFileByName_Viewer_(rootFolder, fileName);

  if (target) {
    target.setContent(compressed);
    return target;
  }
  return rootFolder.createFile(fileName, compressed, MimeType.PLAIN_TEXT);
}

function updatePl01IndexInternal_(fileId, manifestOrJsonString, optMeta) {
  if (!fileId) return false;
  const meta = optMeta || resolveContractMetaForFileId_(fileId);
  if (!meta.maHD || /^PL\d+/i.test(meta.maHD)) return false;

  const manifest = (typeof manifestOrJsonString === "string")
    ? JSON.parse(decompressGzipString_Viewer_(manifestOrJsonString))
    : manifestOrJsonString;

  const pages = extractCleanPl01PagesFromManifest_(manifest);
  const loaded = loadPl01IndexObj_(meta.year);
  const indexObj = loaded.indexObj;

  if (pages.length > 0) {
    indexObj.files[fileId] = {
      maHD: meta.maHD,
      apStart: Number(manifest.appendixStartPage) || pages[0][0],
      pages: pages
    };
  } else {
    delete indexObj.files[fileId];
  }

  savePl01IndexObj_(meta.year, indexObj, loaded.file);
  return true;
}

function getPl01IndexMeta() {
  const rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(getIndexRootFolderId_Viewer_());
  const files = rootFolder.getFiles();
  const list = [];

  while (files.hasNext()) {
    const f = files.next();
    if (f.isTrashed()) continue;
    const m = f.getName().match(/^Index_PL01_(\d{4})\.json$/);
    if (!m) continue;
    list.push({
      year: m[1],
      fileId: f.getId(),
      updatedAt: f.getLastUpdated().getTime(),
      size: f.getSize()
    });
  }

  list.sort((a, b) => Number(b.year) - Number(a.year));
  return list;
}

function getPl01IndexByYear(year) {
  const y = String(year || "").trim();
  if (!/^\d{4}$/.test(y)) throw new Error("Năm Index không hợp lệ.");

  const rootFolder = DriveApp.getFolderById(getIndexRootFolderId_Viewer_());
  const file = findFileByName_Viewer_(rootFolder, getIndexFileName_Viewer_(y));
  if (!file) {
    return { year: y, found: false, updatedAt: 0, data: null };
  }

  return {
    year: y,
    found: true,
    fileId: file.getId(),
    updatedAt: file.getLastUpdated().getTime(),
    data: file.getBlob().getDataAsString("UTF-8")
  };
}

function backfillPl01IndexFromDrive(options) {
  const startMs = Date.now();
  const opts = options || {};
  const forceRebuild = Boolean(opts.forceRebuild);
  const targetYear = opts.targetYear ? String(opts.targetYear).trim() : "";

  const res = Sheets.Spreadsheets.Values.get(SHEET_ID, "SO HDTCXD BCONS - NTP!B3:P", {
    valueRenderOption: "FORMATTED_VALUE"
  });
  const rows = res.values || [];
  const byYear = {};

  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
    const r = rows[i];
    const rawDate = String(r[0] || "").trim();
    const maHD = String(r[3] || "").trim();
    const scanRaw = String(r[14] || "").trim();

    if (!maHD || /^PL\d+/i.test(maHD) || !scanRaw || !scanRaw.includes("|")) continue;

    const entries = scanRaw.split(";;").filter(Boolean);
    let mainEntry = null;
    for (let j = 0; j < entries.length; j++) {
      const parts = entries[j].split("|");
      const fId = (parts[0] || "").trim();
      const fName = (parts[1] || "").trim();
      const digitizedFlag = (parts[2] || "").trim();
      if (!fId || digitizedFlag !== "1" || isPlOrPdgFile_Viewer_(fName)) continue;
      mainEntry = { fileId: fId, fileName: fName };
      break;
    }
    if (!mainEntry) continue;

    const year = extractYearFromContract_Viewer_(maHD, rawDate, mainEntry.fileId);
    if (targetYear && year !== targetYear) continue;

    if (!byYear[year]) byYear[year] = [];
    byYear[year].push({ fileId: mainEntry.fileId, fileName: mainEntry.fileName, maHD: maHD });
  }

  let processed = 0, skipped = 0, failed = 0, timedOut = false;
  const yearsUpdated = [];

  const years = Object.keys(byYear).sort((a, b) => Number(b) - Number(a));
  for (let yIdx = 0; yIdx < years.length; yIdx++) {
    if (timedOut) break;
    const y = years[yIdx];
    const items = byYear[y];

    const loaded = loadPl01IndexObj_(y);
    const workingFiles = forceRebuild ? {} : Object.assign({}, loaded.indexObj.files);
    const touchedIds = {};
    let changed = forceRebuild;

    for (let k = 0; k < items.length; k++) {
      if (Date.now() - startMs > 270000) {
        timedOut = true;
        break;
      }

      const item = items[k];
      if (!forceRebuild && workingFiles[item.fileId]) {
        skipped++;
        continue;
      }

      try {
        const folder = getFolderForFile_Viewer_(item.fileId);
        const jsonName = getJsonCacheName_Viewer_(item.fileName);
        const cleanBase = item.fileName.normalize("NFC").replace(/\.pdf$/i, "").trim();

        let jsonFile = findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, jsonName) ||
                       findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, jsonName.normalize("NFD"));
        if (!jsonFile && cleanBase) {
          jsonFile = findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, cleanBase + ".json") ||
                     findFileByName_Viewer_(folder, (cleanBase + ".json").normalize("NFD"));
        }

        if (!jsonFile) {
          failed++;
          continue;
        }

        const rawContent = jsonFile.getBlob().getDataAsString("UTF-8");
        const manifest = JSON.parse(decompressGzipString_Viewer_(rawContent));
        const pages = extractCleanPl01PagesFromManifest_(manifest);

        const entryObj = {
          maHD: item.maHD,
          apStart: Number(manifest.appendixStartPage) || (pages.length > 0 ? pages[0][0] : 0),
          pages: pages
        };
        workingFiles[item.fileId] = entryObj;
        touchedIds[item.fileId] = entryObj;
        changed = true;
        processed++;
      } catch (itemErr) {
        failed++;
        console.warn(`[Backfill] Lỗi file ${item.fileName} (${item.fileId}): ${itemErr.message}`);
      }
    }

    if (changed) {
      const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
      try {
        lock.waitLock(20000);
        const latest = loadPl01IndexObj_(y);
        const finalFiles = forceRebuild ? workingFiles : Object.assign({}, latest.indexObj.files, touchedIds);
        latest.indexObj.files = finalFiles;
        savePl01IndexObj_(y, latest.indexObj, latest.file);
        yearsUpdated.push(y);
      } finally {
        lock.releaseLock();
      }
    }
  }

  const summary = { processed, skipped, failed, timedOut, yearsUpdated };
  console.log("[Backfill PL01 Index] Summary:", JSON.stringify(summary));
  return summary;
}

function rebuildAllPl01IndexesFromDrive() {
  return backfillPl01IndexFromDrive({ forceRebuild: true });
}

const SHEET_ID = "1y73SqPevgBN7s0_uhUhYQoGD1OXDbDgvHHXbLQD1EH0";
const SHEET_NAME_TBKQ = "TBKQTT";
