  function callVisionApiBatchAsync(imagesBase64Array) {
    return new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
      googleScriptRun
        .withSuccessHandler(resolve)
        .withFailureHandler(reject)
        .callCloudVisionApiBatch(imagesBase64Array);
    });
  }

  function isScannedPageBlank(srcCanvas) {
    const w = srcCanvas.width;
    const h = srcCanvas.height;
    if (w === 0 || h === 0) return true;

    const sw = 120;
    const sh = 160;
    const sampleCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    sampleCanvas.width = sw;
    sampleCanvas.height = sh;
    const sCtx = sampleCanvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
    sCtx.drawImage(srcCanvas, 0, 0, sw, sh);

    const imgData = sCtx.getImageData(0, 0, sw, sh);
    const d = imgData.data;

    const marginX = Math.round(sw * 0.05);
    const marginY = Math.round(sh * 0.05);

    let darkPixels = 0;
    let totalExamined = 0;

    for (let y = marginY; y < sh - marginY; y++) {
      for (let x = marginX; x < sw - marginX; x++) {
        const idx = (y * sw + x) * 4;
        const gray = d[idx] * 0.299 + d[idx + 1] * 0.587 + d[idx + 2] * 0.114;
        if (gray < 180) darkPixels++;
        totalExamined++;
      }
    }

    const inkRatio = (darkPixels / Math.max(1, totalExamined)) * 100;
    return inkRatio < 0.12;
  }

  async function ensurePdfLibLoaded() {
    if (window.PDFLib) return window.PDFLib;
    await new Promise(function(resolve, reject) {
      const script = document.createElement("script");
      script.src = "https://unpkg.com/pdf-lib/dist/pdf-lib.min.js";
      script.onload = resolve;
      script.onerror = () => reject(new Error("Không thể tải thư viện xử lý PDF (pdf-lib)."));
      document.head.appendChild(script);
    });
    return window.PDFLib;
  }

  function arrayBufferToBase64(buffer) {
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(buffer);
    const CHUNK = 0x8000;
    const parts = [];
    for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += CHUNK) {
      parts.push(String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, i + CHUNK)));
    }
    return btoa(parts.join(""));
  }

  async function verifyDriveFileSizeMatches(fileId, expectedBytes) {
    try {
      const token = await getDriveTokenAsync();
      const slashes = String.fromCharCode(47, 47);
      const metaUrl = "https:" + slashes + "www.googleapis.com/drive/v3/files/" + encodeURIComponent(fileId) + "?fields=size&supportsAllDrives=true";
      const res = await fetch(metaUrl, { headers: { Authorization: "Bearer " + token } });
      if (!res.ok) return false;
      const meta = await res.json();
      return Number(meta.size || 0) === expectedBytes;
    } catch (e) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  async function overwriteOriginalPdfOnDrive(fileId, cleanBuffer) {
    if (!fileId || String(fileId).startsWith("local_") || !cleanBuffer) return false;
    if (typeof callBackend !== "function") return false;

    const totalSize = cleanBuffer.byteLength;

    // LUỒNG CHÍNH: Xin Resumable PATCH Session URL -> Đẩy thẳng nhị phân lên Drive CDN
    try {
      const session = await callBackend("getPdfOverwriteSession_Backend", [
        fileId,
        totalSize,
        window.location.origin || ""
      ]);

      if (session && session.success && session.uploadUrl) {
        const chunkSize = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
        const totalChunks = Math.ceil(totalSize / chunkSize);
        let hitCorsBypass = false;

        for (let i = 0; i < totalChunks; i++) {
          const start = i * chunkSize;
          const end = Math.min(start + chunkSize, totalSize);
          const chunk = cleanBuffer.slice(start, end);

          try {
            const resp = await fetch(session.uploadUrl, {
              method: "PUT",
              headers: {
                "Content-Type": "application/pdf",
                "Content-Range": `bytes ${start}-${end - 1}/${totalSize}`
              },
              body: chunk
            });

            if (resp.status !== 308 && resp.status !== 200 && resp.status !== 201) {
              throw new Error("Drive phản hồi HTTP " + resp.status);
            }
          } catch (fetchErr) {
            const isLastChunk = (i === totalChunks - 1);
            const isCorsBlock = fetchErr.name === "TypeError" || (fetchErr.message && fetchErr.message.includes("Failed to fetch"));
            if (isLastChunk && isCorsBlock) {
              hitCorsBypass = true;
              break;
            }
            throw fetchErr;
          }
        }

        // Nếu bị trình duyệt chặn đọc phản hồi cuối do CORS -> kiểm chứng dung lượng thật trên Drive
        if (hitCorsBypass) {
          await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 350));
          const verified = await verifyDriveFileSizeMatches(fileId, totalSize);
          if (!verified) {
            throw new Error("Xác thực dung lượng sau ghi đè không khớp (Drive chưa nhận file mới).");
          }
        }
        return true;
      }
    } catch (resumableErr) {
      console.warn("Resumable PATCH lỗi, thử dự phòng cho file nhỏ:", resumableErr);
    }

    // DỰ PHÒNG (chỉ cho file <= 3MB)
    if (totalSize <= 3 * 1024 * 1024) {
      const b64 = arrayBufferToBase64(cleanBuffer);
      const res = await callBackend("overwritePdfFile_Backend", [fileId, b64]);
      return Boolean(res && res.success);
    }

    return false;
  }

  async function syncManifestIfStaleFromLocal(fileId, pdf, pdfByteLength, epoch) {
    if (!S.currentManifest || !pdf) return;
    const m = S.currentManifest;
    const sizeMismatch = Boolean(m.pdfByteLength && pdfByteLength && m.pdfByteLength !== pdfByteLength);
    const pageMismatch = Boolean(m.numPages && pdf.numPages !== m.numPages);

    if (!sizeMismatch && !pageMismatch) return;

    try {
      const res = await fetchDriveCacheAdaptive(fileId, S.currentFileName);
      if (epoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;
      if (res && res.found && res.data) {
        const freshManifest = await ST.decompressManifest(res.data);
        if (epoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;
        S.currentManifest = freshManifest;
        ST.putSessionCache(fileId, freshManifest);
        ST.saveLocalManifest(fileId, freshManifest);
        console.log("%c🔄 [SYNC] Đã đồng bộ lại Manifest mới nhất từ Drive (khắc phục lệch IndexedDB giữa các máy).", "color: #00e5ff;");
      }
    } catch (syncErr) {
      console.warn("Không thể đồng bộ lại Manifest mới từ Drive:", syncErr);
    }
  }

  async function ensurePdfPreloaded(fileId, endPct = 95, isFromLocalCache = false) {
    if (S.currentPdfDoc) {
      U.stopSoftProgress();
      if (S.transitionWaitPromise) {
        await S.transitionWaitPromise;
        if (fileId !== S.currentFileId) throw new Error("ABORTED");
      }
      U.updateLoaderProgress(98);
      return S.currentPdfDoc;
    }
    const epoch = S.currentSessionEpoch;

    let localBuf = await ST.getLocalPdfBinary(fileId);
    if (localBuf && S.currentManifest && S.currentManifest.pdfByteLength && localBuf.byteLength !== S.currentManifest.pdfByteLength) {
      await Promise.all([ST.deleteLocalPdfBinary(fileId), ST.clearFilePageBitmaps(fileId)]);
      localBuf = null;
    }
    let pdfData = localBuf;

    if (!pdfData) {
      const ov = document.getElementById("loading-overlay");
      if (!ov || ov.style.display !== "flex") {
        U.showLoading("LOADING DOCUMENT", 5);
      }
      U.startSoftProgress(Math.max(5, S.currentLoaderPct), 75, 0.015);

      let streamStartPct = null;
      pdfData = await fetchPdfDirectOrFallback(fileId, (loaded, total) => {
        if (epoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;
        U.stopSoftProgress();
        if (streamStartPct === null) {
          streamStartPct = S.currentLoaderPct;
        }
        const ratio = total > 0
          ? Math.min(1, loaded / total)
          : Math.min(0.92, 1 - Math.exp(-loaded / (8 * 1024 * 1024)));
        const pct = streamStartPct + ratio * (endPct - streamStartPct);
        if (pct > S.currentLoaderPct) {
          U.updateLoaderProgress(pct);
        }
      });
    }

    U.stopSoftProgress();
    if (epoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) throw new Error("ABORTED");

    if (S.transitionWaitPromise) {
      await S.transitionWaitPromise;
      if (epoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) throw new Error("ABORTED");
    }

    U.updateLoaderProgress(96);
    const preservedBuffer = pdfData.slice(0);
    const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: pdfData }).promise;
    if (epoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) {
      try { pdf.destroy(); } catch (e) {}
      throw new Error("ABORTED");
    }

    // Nếu Manifest đang đọc từ IndexedDB cục bộ bị lệch với PDF thật trên Drive -> kéo lại JSON mới từ Drive (KHÔNG số hóa lại)
    if (isFromLocalCache && !isPlOrPdgFile(S.currentFileName)) {
      await syncManifestIfStaleFromLocal(fileId, pdf, preservedBuffer.byteLength, epoch);
      if (epoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) {
        try { pdf.destroy(); } catch (e) {}
        throw new Error("ABORTED");
      }
    }

    S.currentPdfDoc = pdf;
    return pdf;
  }

  async function scanAppendixWithVision(pdf, ctx, canvas, skippedBlankPages) {
    const SCAN_AI_SCALE = 2.85;
    const VISION_PAGE_BUDGET = 16;
    let visionApiPages = 0;
    let appendixStartPage = -1;
    const appendixPages = [];

    for (let pTop = pdf.numPages; pTop >= 1; pTop -= 2) {
      if (visionApiPages >= VISION_PAGE_BUDGET) break;

      const candidateNums = [pTop];
      if (pTop - 1 >= 1) candidateNums.push(pTop - 1);

      const scanPct = 20 + Math.round(((pdf.numPages - pTop + 1) / Math.min(pdf.numPages, VISION_PAGE_BUDGET)) * 22);
      U.updateProcessProgress(Math.min(42, scanPct), "Đang số hóa tài liệu...");
      const pairData = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < candidateNums.length; i++) {
        const pNum = candidateNums[i];
        const page = await pdf.getPage(pNum);
        const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: SCAN_AI_SCALE });

        canvas.width = viewport.width;
        canvas.height = viewport.height;
        await page.render({ canvasContext: ctx, viewport: viewport, intent: "print" }).promise;
        page.cleanup();

        if (isScannedPageBlank(canvas)) {
          skippedBlankPages.add(pNum);
          continue;
        }
        if (visionApiPages >= VISION_PAGE_BUDGET) break;

        const aiJpegImg = canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.92);
        const pageData = {
          pageNum: pNum,
          pdfPageNum: pNum,
          width: U.round1(viewport.width / SCAN_AI_SCALE),
          height: U.round1(viewport.height / SCAN_AI_SCALE),
          textItems: []
        };

        pairData.push({ pNum: pNum, pageData: pageData, jpeg: aiJpegImg });
        visionApiPages++;
      }

      if (pairData.length > 0) {
        const visionResults = await callVisionApiBatchAsync(pairData.map(item => item.jpeg));

        let foundBreak = false;
        for (let i = 0; i < pairData.length; i++) {
          const item = pairData[i];
          const visionRes = visionResults[i];

          if (visionRes) {
            const rot = visionRes.rotation || 0;
            item.pageData.rotation = rot;

            if (rot === 90 || rot === 270) {
              const origW = item.pageData.width;
              item.pageData.width = item.pageData.height;
              item.pageData.height = origW;
            }

            if (visionRes.words && visionRes.words.length > 0) {
              item.pageData.textItems = visionRes.words.map(function(w) {
                return [
                  w.str.trim(),
                  U.round1(w.x / SCAN_AI_SCALE),
                  U.round1(item.pageData.height - ((w.y + w.h) / SCAN_AI_SCALE)),
                  U.round1(w.w / SCAN_AI_SCALE),
                  U.round1(w.h / SCAN_AI_SCALE)
                ];
              });
            }
          }

          appendixPages.push(item.pageData);
          const aiRawText = item.pageData.textItems.map(it => it[0] || "").join(" ");
          const aiClean = SR.cleanNaturalToken(aiRawText);

          if (aiClean.includes("phuluchopdongso01") || aiClean.includes("phuluchopdongso1")) {
            appendixStartPage = item.pNum;
            foundBreak = true;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (foundBreak) break;
      }
    }

    if (appendixStartPage === -1) {
      appendixStartPage = pdf.numPages + 1;
    }

    return { appendixStartPage, appendixPages };
  }

  // =========================================================================
  // PIPELINE SỐ HÓA JIT & LÀM SẠCH FILE GỐC
  // =========================================================================
  function processAndOptimizeFileJIT(fileId, localFileObj = null, preloadedBuffer = null) {
    const isLocalUpload = (localFileObj !== null);
    const uploadFileName = S.currentFileName;
    const myEpoch = S.currentSessionEpoch;
    U.setAntiExitGuard(true);

    const timeStartTotal = performance.now();
    let timeStageLap = timeStartTotal;

    const getLapTime = () => {
      const now = performance.now();
      const duration = ((now - timeStageLap) / 1000).toFixed(2);
      timeStageLap = now;
      return duration + "s";
    };

    const getTotalTime = () => ((performance.now() - timeStartTotal) / 1000).toFixed(2) + "s";

    console.log(`%c======================================================`, "color: #FFBA08; font-weight: bold;");
    console.log(`%c🚀 BẮT ĐẦU SỐ HÓA: "${uploadFileName}"`, "color: #FFBA08; font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold;");
    console.log(`%c======================================================`, "color: #FFBA08; font-weight: bold;");

    const loadingTitleEl = document.getElementById("loading-text");
    if (loadingTitleEl) loadingTitleEl.textContent = "DIGITIZING DOCUMENT";

    U.updateProcessProgress(Math.max(5, Math.round(S.currentLoaderPct)), "Đang chuẩn bị tài liệu...");

    const dlStartPct = Math.max(5, Math.round(S.currentLoaderPct));
    const dataSourcePromise = isLocalUpload
      ? localFileObj.arrayBuffer()
      : (preloadedBuffer
          ? Promise.resolve(preloadedBuffer)
          : fetchPdfDirectOrFallback(fileId, (loaded, total) => {
              if (myEpoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch || !total) return;
              const dlPct = dlStartPct + Math.round((loaded / total) * Math.max(1, 20 - dlStartPct));
              U.updateProcessProgress(Math.min(20, dlPct), "Đang chuẩn bị tài liệu...");
            }));

    dataSourcePromise
      .then(async function(rawPdfData) {
        let canvas = null;
        let ctx = null;
        try {
          const preservedPdfData = rawPdfData.slice(0);
          const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: rawPdfData }).promise;
          const sourcePageCount = pdf.numPages;

          console.log(
            `%c[1/4] KHỞI TẠO XONG: Nạp thành công file nhị phân (${sourcePageCount} trang, ${(preservedPdfData.byteLength / 1048576).toFixed(2)} MB) ⏱ Thời gian: ${getLapTime()}`,
            "color: #00e5ff; font-weight: bold;"
          );

          const manifest = { fileId: fileId, numPages: sourcePageCount, pages: [] };
          canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
          ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });
          const skippedBlankPages = new Set();

          // GIAI ĐOẠN 2: Quét phụ lục bằng Vision API
          U.updateProcessProgress(20, "Đang số hóa tài liệu...");
          const { appendixStartPage, appendixPages } = await scanAppendixWithVision(pdf, ctx, canvas, skippedBlankPages);
          manifest.pages.push(...appendixPages);
          manifest.appendixStartPage = appendixStartPage;
          manifest.sourcePageCount = sourcePageCount;

          const rotatedCount = appendixPages.filter(p => p.rotation && p.rotation !== 0).length;
          console.log(
            `%c[2/4] VISION AI XONG: Tìm thấy Phụ lục từ Trang ${appendixStartPage} (Đã xoay: ${rotatedCount} trang, Bỏ trắng: ${skippedBlankPages.size} trang) ⏱ Thời gian: ${getLapTime()}`,
            "color: #69f0ae; font-weight: bold;"
          );

          // GIAI ĐOẠN 3: Thân Hợp Đồng + Lọc trang trắng (scale 0.22)
          U.updateProcessProgress(45, "Đang xử lý tài liệu...");
          const contractEndPage = appendixStartPage - 1;
          const preScannedPages = new Set(appendixPages.map(p => p.pageNum));
          const bodyPages = [];

          for (let pageNum = 1; pageNum <= contractEndPage; pageNum++) {
            if (preScannedPages.has(pageNum)) continue;

            const page = await pdf.getPage(pageNum);
            const viewport = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.0 });

            let textItems = [];
            try {
              const tc = await page.getTextContent();
              if (tc && tc.items) {
                textItems = tc.items.map(it => [
                  (it.str || "").trim(),
                  U.round1(it.transform[4]),
                  U.round1(it.transform[5]),
                  U.round1(it.width),
                  U.round1(it.height)
                ]).filter(it => it[0].length > 0);
              }
            } catch (tcErr) {}

            if (textItems.length === 0) {
              const thumbVp = page.getViewport({ scale: 0.22 });
              canvas.width = thumbVp.width;
              canvas.height = thumbVp.height;
              await page.render({ canvasContext: ctx, viewport: thumbVp, intent: "display" }).promise;
              if (isScannedPageBlank(canvas)) {
                skippedBlankPages.add(pageNum);
                page.cleanup();
                continue;
              }
            }

            bodyPages.push({
              pageNum: pageNum,
              pdfPageNum: pageNum,
              width: U.round1(viewport.width),
              height: U.round1(viewport.height),
              rotation: 0,
              textItems: textItems
            });

            page.cleanup();

            if (pageNum % 5 === 0) {
              const pct = 45 + Math.round((pageNum / Math.max(1, contractEndPage)) * 35);
              U.updateProcessProgress(pct, "Đang xử lý tài liệu...");
            }
          }

          manifest.pages.push(...bodyPages);
          manifest.pages.sort((a, b) => a.pageNum - b.pageNum);

          console.log(
            `%c[3/4] XỬ LÝ THÂN HĐ XONG: (${bodyPages.length} trang) ⏱ Thời gian: ${getLapTime()}`,
            "color: #ffd740; font-weight: bold;"
          );

          // GIAI ĐOẠN 4: Sửa trực tiếp trang trắng & góc xoay vào file PDF gốc trên Drive
          const rotationMap = new Map();
          manifest.pages.forEach(p => {
            if (p.rotation && p.rotation !== 0) rotationMap.set(p.pageNum, p.rotation);
          });

          const needsPhysicalFix = !isLocalUpload && (skippedBlankPages.size > 0 || rotationMap.size > 0);
          let activePdf = pdf;
          let activePdfBuffer = preservedPdfData;
          let fixedOriginalOnDrive = false;

          if (needsPhysicalFix) {
            try {
              U.updateProcessProgress(82, "Đang tối ưu tài liệu...");
              const PDFLib = await ensurePdfLibLoaded();
              const editDoc = await PDFLib.PDFDocument.load(preservedPdfData.slice(0), { ignoreEncryption: true });

              rotationMap.forEach((extraRot, origPageNum) => {
                const idx = origPageNum - 1;
                if (idx >= 0 && idx < editDoc.getPageCount()) {
                  const p = editDoc.getPage(idx);
                  const curRot = p.getRotation().angle || 0;
                  p.setRotation(PDFLib.degrees((curRot + extraRot) % 360));
                }
              });

              const blankDesc = Array.from(skippedBlankPages).sort((a, b) => b - a);
              blankDesc.forEach(origPageNum => {
                const idx = origPageNum - 1;
                if (idx >= 0 && idx < editDoc.getPageCount() && editDoc.getPageCount() > 1) {
                  editDoc.removePage(idx);
                }
              });

              const cleanBytes = await editDoc.save();
              const cleanBuffer = cleanBytes.buffer.slice(cleanBytes.byteOffset, cleanBytes.byteOffset + cleanBytes.byteLength);

              U.updateProcessProgress(86, "Đang lưu dữ liệu...");
              fixedOriginalOnDrive = await overwriteOriginalPdfOnDrive(fileId, cleanBuffer);

              if (fixedOriginalOnDrive) {
                activePdfBuffer = cleanBuffer;
                await ST.clearFilePageBitmaps(fileId);
                await ST.saveLocalPdfBinary(fileId, cleanBuffer);
                const newPdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: cleanBuffer.slice(0) }).promise;
                try { activePdf.destroy(); } catch (e) {}
                activePdf = newPdf;
              }
            } catch (repairErr) {
              console.warn("Không thể ghi đè file PDF sạch, dùng chế độ ánh xạ ảo dự phòng:", repairErr);
              fixedOriginalOnDrive = false;
            }
          }

          const cleanPages = [];
          let cleanAppendixPage = -1;
          const foundIdx = manifest.pages.findIndex(p => p.pageNum >= appendixStartPage);
          if (foundIdx !== -1) cleanAppendixPage = foundIdx + 1;

          manifest.pages.forEach((p, idx) => {
            cleanPages.push({
              pageNum: idx + 1,
              pdfPageNum: fixedOriginalOnDrive ? (idx + 1) : p.pageNum,
              width: p.width,
              height: p.height,
              rotation: fixedOriginalOnDrive ? 0 : (p.rotation || 0),
              textItems: p.textItems
            });
          });

          manifest.pages = cleanPages;
          manifest.numPages = cleanPages.length;
          manifest.appendixStartPage = (cleanAppendixPage !== -1) ? cleanAppendixPage : (cleanPages.length + 1);
          manifest.mapsOriginalPdf = true;
          manifest.pdfByteLength = activePdfBuffer.byteLength;
          manifest.originalPdfCleanChecked = true;

          if (needsPhysicalFix && !fixedOriginalOnDrive) {
            manifest.pdfRepairFailed = true;
          } else {
            delete manifest.pdfRepairFailed;
          }

          U.updateProcessProgress(90, "Đang lưu dữ liệu...");
          let finalDriveFileId = fileId;
          let finalSizeStr = (activePdfBuffer.byteLength / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(2) + " MB";

          const compressedPayload = await ST.compressManifest(manifest);
          await saveDriveCacheAdaptive(finalDriveFileId, uploadFileName, compressedPayload);

          ST.putSessionCache(finalDriveFileId, manifest);
          await ST.saveLocalManifest(finalDriveFileId, manifest);

          U.updateProcessProgress(96, "Đang hoàn tất...");
          if (cleanPages.length >= 1) {
            const firstPdfPageNum = cleanPages[0].pdfPageNum || 1;
            const page1 = await activePdf.getPage(firstPdfPageNum);
            const rawDpr = window.devicePixelRatio || 1;
            const sharpFactor = Math.max(rawDpr, 2.0);
            const effectiveScale = Math.min(S.cachedRenderScale * sharpFactor, 2.0);
            const effectiveRot1 = (page1.rotate + (cleanPages[0].rotation || 0)) % 360;
            const vp = page1.getViewport({ scale: effectiveScale, rotation: effectiveRot1 });

            const bCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
            bCanvas.width = Math.floor(vp.width);
            bCanvas.height = Math.floor(vp.height);
            const bCtx = bCanvas.getContext("2d");
            bCtx.fillStyle = "#ffffff";
            bCtx.fillRect(0, 0, bCanvas.width, bCanvas.height);
            await page1.render({ canvasContext: bCtx, viewport: vp, intent: "display" }).promise;
            page1.cleanup();
            await ST.savePageBitmap(finalDriveFileId, firstPdfPageNum, S.cachedRenderScale, bCanvas);
            bCanvas.width = 0;
            bCanvas.height = 0;
          }

          if (S.currentPdfDoc && S.currentPdfDoc !== activePdf) {
            try { S.currentPdfDoc.destroy(); } catch (e) {}
          }
          S.currentPdfDoc = activePdf;

          console.log(
            `%c[4/4] LƯU DRIVE & CACHE 100% XONG: Dung lượng ${finalSizeStr} ⏱ Thời gian: ${getLapTime()}`,
            "color: #e040fb; font-weight: bold;"
          );

          U.updateProcessProgress(100, "Số hóa hoàn tất", true);

          if (typeof window.markScanFileDigitized_PL === "function") {
            window.markScanFileDigitized_PL(finalDriveFileId);
          }

          if (!isLocalUpload && !isPlOrPdgFile(uploadFileName) && BV.pl01Index && typeof BV.pl01Index.sync === "function") {
            setTimeout(() => BV.pl01Index.sync(true), 300);
          }

          if (myEpoch === S.currentSessionEpoch) {
            S.currentManifest = manifest;
            await BV.ui.renderSlicedDocument();
            if (BV.ui.applyPl01SearchContextIfAny) {
              await BV.ui.applyPl01SearchContextIfAny();
            }
          }

          const listBlank = Array.from(skippedBlankPages).sort((a, b) => a - b);
          const listRotated = appendixPages.filter(p => p.rotation && p.rotation !== 0).map(p => `Trang ${p.pageNum} (xoay ${p.rotation}°)`);

          console.log(`%c======================================================`, "color: #00e676; font-weight: bold;");
          console.log(`%c✔ TOÀN BỘ TIẾN TRÌNH HOÀN THÀNH XUẤT SẮC TRONG: ${getTotalTime()}`, "color: #00e676; font-size: 14px; font-weight: bold;");
          console.log(`%c======================================================`, "color: #00e676; font-weight: bold;");
          console.table({
            "Tên tài liệu": uploadFileName,
            "Tổng thời gian": getTotalTime(),
            "Tổng số trang gốc": sourcePageCount + " trang",
            "Tổng số trang sạch (sau xử lý)": cleanPages.length + " trang",
            "Các trang trắng đã loại bỏ": listBlank.length > 0 ? listBlank.join(", ") : "Không có",
            "Các trang phụ lục đã xoay ngang": listRotated.length > 0 ? listRotated.join(", ") : "Không có",
            "Đã sửa trực tiếp file gốc Drive": fixedOriginalOnDrive ? "Có" : "Không (không cần hoặc không đủ quyền)",
            "Dung lượng bản PDF sạch": finalSizeStr,
            "Drive File ID": finalDriveFileId
          });

        } catch (err) {
          U.setAntiExitGuard(false);
          U.hideLoading();
          U.updateProcessProgress(0, "Số hóa thất bại", false, true);
          console.error(`%c❌ LỖI TRONG TIẾN TRÌNH: ${err.message}`, "color: #ff1744; font-size: 13px; font-weight: bold;", err);
        } finally {
          if (canvas) {
            canvas.width = 0;
            canvas.height = 0;
          }
        }
      })
      .catch(function(err) {
        U.setAntiExitGuard(false);
        U.hideLoading();
        console.error(`%c❌ LỖI TẢI NHỊ PHÂN: ${err.message}`, "color: #ff1744; font-weight: bold;", err);

        const vp = document.getElementById("viewport-container");
        if (vp) {
          vp.style.visibility = "visible";
          vp.innerHTML = `
            <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100%; width: 100%; text-align: center; padding: 30px; box-sizing: border-box;">
              <i class="bi bi-file-earmark-x" style="font-size: 55px; color: #ff4d4d; margin-bottom: 15px; opacity: 0.85;"></i>
              <h4 style="color: #ff4d4d; font-size: 15px; font-weight: bold; margin: 0 0 10px 0; text-transform: uppercase;">KHÔNG THỂ MỞ TỆP TIN</h4>
              <p style="color: #95A1AF; font-size: 12px; line-height: 1.6; max-width: 450px; margin: 0 0 20px 0;">
                Tệp có thể đã bị xóa trên Google Drive hoặc tài khoản không có quyền truy cập.<br>
                <span style="color: #FFBA08; font-family: monospace; font-size: 11px;">(${err.message || "Lỗi tải tệp"})</span>
              </p>
              <button type="button" class="prv-btn" onclick="window.BconsViewer.loadFile('${fileId}', '${(uploadFileName || "").replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" style="padding: 6px 16px; font-size: 12px;">
                <i class="bi bi-arrow-clockwise" style="margin-right: 6px;"></i> THỬ LẠI
              </button>
            </div>
          `;
        }
        if (typeof showToast_PL === "function") {
          showToast_PL("⚠️ Không thể tải file: " + err.message, "error");
        } else {
          alert("Lỗi tải PDF: " + err.message);
        }
      });
  }

  function isPlOrPdgFile(fileName) {
    if (!fileName) return false;
    const clean = fileName.toLowerCase();
    const isPdg = /pdg|pđg|phe\s*duyet\s*gia|phê\s*duyệt\s*giá|(\b|_)gia(\b|_)|(\b|_)giá(\b|_)/i.test(clean);
    const isPl = /(^|[^a-z0-9])pl(\d+)?([^a-z0-9]|$)|phu\s*luc|phụ\s*lục/i.test(clean);
    return isPdg || isPl;
  }

  async function loadPdfDirectWithoutOcr(fileId, localFileObj = null) {
    const myEpoch = S.currentSessionEpoch;
    try {
      const ramCached = ST.getSessionCache(fileId);
      if (ramCached) {
        S.currentManifest = ramCached;
        if (S.transitionWaitPromise) await S.transitionWaitPromise;
        if (myEpoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;
        await BV.ui.renderSlicedDocument();
        U.hideLoading();
        return;
      }
      const localCached = await ST.getLocalManifest(fileId);
      if (localCached) {
        ST.putSessionCache(fileId, localCached);
        S.currentManifest = localCached;
        if (S.transitionWaitPromise) await S.transitionWaitPromise;
        if (myEpoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;
        await BV.ui.renderSlicedDocument();
        U.hideLoading();
        return;
      }

      U.startSoftProgress(0, 25, 0.035);
      let streamStartPct = null;
      const rawPdfData = localFileObj
        ? await localFileObj.arrayBuffer()
        : await fetchPdfDirectOrFallback(fileId, (loaded, total) => {
            if (myEpoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;
            U.stopSoftProgress();
            if (streamStartPct === null) streamStartPct = S.currentLoaderPct;
            const ratio = total > 0 ? Math.min(1, loaded / total) : 0;
            const pct = streamStartPct + ratio * (95 - streamStartPct);
            if (pct > S.currentLoaderPct) U.updateLoaderProgress(pct);
          });
      U.stopSoftProgress();
      U.updateLoaderProgress(96);

      if (S.transitionWaitPromise) await S.transitionWaitPromise;
      if (myEpoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;

      const preservedPdfData = rawPdfData.slice(0);
      const pdf = await pdfjsLib.getDocument({ data: rawPdfData }).promise;
      S.currentPdfDoc = pdf;

      const manifest = {
        fileId: fileId,
        numPages: pdf.numPages,
        appendixStartPage: pdf.numPages + 1,
        pages: []
      };

      for (let pNum = 1; pNum <= pdf.numPages; pNum++) {
        const page = await pdf.getPage(pNum);
        const vp = page.getViewport({ scale: 1.0 });

        let textItems = [];
        try {
          const tc = await page.getTextContent();
          if (tc && tc.items) {
            textItems = tc.items.map(it => [
              (it.str || "").trim(),
              U.round1(it.transform[4]),
              U.round1(it.transform[5]),
              U.round1(it.width),
              U.round1(it.height)
            ]).filter(it => it[0].length > 0);
          }
        } catch (tcErr) {}

        manifest.pages.push({
          pageNum: pNum,
          pdfPageNum: pNum,
          width: U.round1(vp.width),
          height: U.round1(vp.height),
          rotation: 0,
          textItems: textItems
        });
        page.cleanup();
      }

      if (myEpoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;

      ST.putSessionCache(fileId, manifest);
      ST.saveLocalManifest(fileId, manifest);
      ST.saveLocalPdfBinary(fileId, preservedPdfData);

      S.currentManifest = manifest;
      await BV.ui.renderSlicedDocument();
      U.hideLoading();

      console.log(`%c⚡ [DIRECT VIEW] Đã mở trực tiếp (không số hóa): "${S.currentFileName}" (${pdf.numPages} trang)`, "color: #00BCD4; font-weight: bold;");
    } catch (err) {
      U.hideLoading();
      console.error("Lỗi mở trực tiếp PDF:", err);
      if (myEpoch === S.currentSessionEpoch) {
        const vp = document.getElementById("viewport-container");
        if (vp) {
          vp.style.visibility = "visible";
          vp.innerHTML = `
            <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100%; width: 100%; text-align: center; padding: 30px; box-sizing: border-box;">
              <i class="bi bi-file-earmark-x" style="font-size: 55px; color: #ff4d4d; margin-bottom: 15px;"></i>
              <h4 style="color: #ff4d4d; font-size: 15px; font-weight: bold; margin: 0 0 10px 0;">KHÔNG THỂ MỞ TỆP TIN</h4>
              <p style="color: #95A1AF; font-size: 12px; margin: 0 0 20px 0;">${err.message || "Lỗi tải tệp"}</p>
            </div>
          `;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  async function loadFile(fileId, fileName, sizeMB, pl01Context = null) {
    if (S.currentFileId === fileId && S.currentManifest) {
      S.pendingPl01Context = pl01Context || null;
      if (BV.ui.applyPl01SearchContextIfAny) await BV.ui.applyPl01SearchContextIfAny();
      return;
    }

    BV.ui.setupSearchFaviconNote();
    BV.ui.setupHandToolPanning();
    BV.ui.setupCtrlWheelZoom();
    BV.ui.setupTouchPinchZoom();
    BV.ui.setupSmartClipboardCopy();

    S.currentSessionEpoch++;
    const myEpoch = S.currentSessionEpoch;

    if (S.activeFetchAbortController) {
      S.activeFetchAbortController.abort();
      S.activeFetchAbortController = null;
    }
    S.activeFetchAbortController = new AbortController();

    if (S.currentPdfDoc) {
      S.currentPdfDoc.destroy();
      S.currentPdfDoc = null;
    }

    S.currentManifest = null;
    S.currentFileId = fileId;
    S.currentFileName = fileName || "Document.pdf";
    S.currentFileSizeMB = (sizeMB !== undefined) ? sizeMB : 0;
    S.currentScale = 1.0;
    S.pendingPl01Context = pl01Context || null;
    S.activePl01Context = null;

    if (BV.ui.resetPl01VariantsUI) BV.ui.resetPl01VariantsUI();

    const fnEl = document.getElementById("preview-file-name") || document.getElementById("prv-file-name");
    if (fnEl) fnEl.textContent = S.currentFileName;

    SR.clearSearchState();
    const searchInp = document.getElementById("ai-search-input");
    if (searchInp && !S.pendingPl01Context) searchInp.value = "";
    const inp = document.getElementById("jump-page-input");
    if (inp) inp.value = "1";

    const vp = document.getElementById("viewport-container");
    if (vp) {
      vp.style.visibility = "hidden";
      vp.innerHTML = "";
    }
    const pageInd = document.getElementById("page-indicator");
    if (pageInd) pageInd.style.display = "none";

    const isDirectDoc = isPlOrPdgFile(S.currentFileName);

    let cachedManifest = ST.getSessionCache(fileId);
    if (!cachedManifest) {
      cachedManifest = await ST.getLocalManifest(fileId);
      if (myEpoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;
      if (cachedManifest) ST.putSessionCache(fileId, cachedManifest);
    }

    if (cachedManifest) {
      S.currentManifest = cachedManifest;
      if (!isDirectDoc && typeof window.markScanFileDigitized_PL === "function") {
        window.markScanFileDigitized_PL(fileId);
      }

      const hasBin = await ST.getLocalPdfBinary(fileId);
      if (myEpoch !== S.currentSessionEpoch) return;

      if (!hasBin || S.isPanelTransitioning) {
        U.showLoading("LOADING DOCUMENT", hasBin ? 80 : 5);
      }

      try {
        await ensurePdfPreloaded(fileId, 95, true);
        await BV.ui.renderSlicedDocument();
        if (BV.ui.applyPl01SearchContextIfAny) {
          await BV.ui.applyPl01SearchContextIfAny();
        }
      } catch (e) {
        if (e.message !== "ABORTED" && e.message !== "REPAIR_OLD_MANIFEST") {
          U.hideLoading();
          if (vp) {
            vp.style.visibility = "visible";
            vp.innerHTML = `
              <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; align-items: center; justify-content: center; height: 100%; width: 100%; text-align: center; padding: 30px; box-sizing: border-box;">
                <i class="bi bi-file-earmark-x" style="font-size: 55px; color: #ff4d4d; margin-bottom: 15px;"></i>
                <h4 style="color: #ff4d4d; font-size: 15px; font-weight: bold; margin: 0 0 10px 0;">KHÔNG THỂ MỞ TỆP TIN</h4>
                <p style="color: #95A1AF; font-size: 12px; margin: 0;">${e.message || "Lỗi tải tệp"}</p>
              </div>
            `;
          }
        }
      }
      return;
    }

    U.showLoading("LOADING DOCUMENT", 0);

    if (isDirectDoc) {
      await loadPdfDirectWithoutOcr(fileId);
      return;
    }

    U.startSoftProgress(0, 12, 0.035);
    checkDriveCacheWithRetry(fileId, S.currentFileName, 1);
  }

  BV.pipeline = {
    getDriveTokenAsync,
    invalidateDriveToken,
    fetchPdfDirectOrFallback,
    processAndOptimizeFileJIT,
    ensurePdfPreloaded,
    isPlOrPdgFile,
    loadFile
  };
})();


=== FILE: public/js/modules/viewer/JS_Viewer_Search.js ===
// =========================================================================
// BCONS DOCUMENT VIEWER - MODULE 2/4: SEARCH ENGINE & HIGHLIGHTING
// =========================================================================

(() => {
  const BV = window.__BconsViewer;
  const S = BV.state;
  const U = BV.utils;

  function isSingleTechnicalCode(query) {
    const trimmed = query.trim();
    if (/\s+/.test(trimmed)) return false;
    const q = trimmed.toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, "");
    const hasLetter = /[A-Z]/.test(q);
    const hasDigit = /[0-9]/.test(q);
    return (hasLetter && hasDigit) || (/^[A-Z]{2,5}$/.test(q) && trimmed === trimmed.toUpperCase());
  }

  function cleanCodeToken(str) {
    if (!str) return "";
    return str.toLowerCase()
              .replace(/\?/g, "7")
              .replace(/0/g, "o")
              .replace(/1/g, "l")
              .replace(/i/g, "l")
              .replace(/5/g, "s")
              .replace(/8/g, "b")
              .replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, "");
  }

  function cleanNaturalToken(str) {
    if (!str) return "";
    return str
      .normalize("NFC")
      .replace(/[\u201C\u201D\u2018\u2019"']/g, "")
      .replace(/²/g, "2")
      .replace(/³/g, "3")
      .replace(/[àáạảãâầấậẩẫăằắặẳẵÀÁẠẢÃÂẦẤẬẨẪĂẰẮẶẲẴ]/g, "a")
      .replace(/[èéẹẻẽêềếệểễÈÉẸẺẼÊỀẾỆỂỄ]/g, "e")
      .replace(/[ìíịỉĩÌÍỊỈĨ]/g, "i")
      .replace(/[òóọỏõôồốộổỗơờớợởỡÒÓỌỎÕÔỒỐỘỔỖƠỜỚỢỞỠ]/g, "o")
      .replace(/[ùúụủũưừứựửữÙÚỤỦŨƯỪỨỰỬỮ]/g, "u")
      .replace(/[ỳýỵỷỹỲÝỴỶỸ]/g, "y")
      .replace(/[đĐ]/g, "d")
      .normalize("NFD")
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/(\d)[.,](?=\d)/g, "$1\uE001")
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\uE001]/g, "")
      .replace(/\uE001/g, ".");
  }

  function cleanSentenceNatural(str) {
    if (!str) return "";
    return str
      .replace(/[àáạảãâầấậẩẫăằắặẳẵÀÁẠẢÃÂẦẤẬẨẪĂẰẮẶẲẴ]/g, "a")
      .replace(/[èéẹẻẽêềếệểễÈÉẸẺẼÊỀẾỆỂỄ]/g, "e")
      .replace(/[ìíịỉĩÌÍỊỈĨ]/g, "i")
      .replace(/[òóọỏõôồốộổỗơờớợởỡÒÓỌỎÕÔỒỐỘỔỖƠỜỚỢỞỠ]/g, "o")
      .replace(/[ùúụủũưừứựửữÙÚỤỦŨƯỪỨỰỬỮ]/g, "u")
      .replace(/[ỳýỵỷỹỲÝỴỶỸ]/g, "y")
      .replace(/[đĐ]/g, "d")
      .normalize("NFD")
      .replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g, "")
      .toLowerCase()
      .replace(/(\d)[.,](?=\d)/g, "$1\uE001")
      .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s\uE001]/g, " ")
      .replace(/\uE001/g, ".")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();
  }

  function isEditDistanceOne(a, b) {
    if (!a || !b) return false;
    if (a === b) return true;
    const la = a.length, lb = b.length;
    if (Math.abs(la - lb) > 1) return false;

    if (Math.min(la, lb) === 1) {
      return la !== lb && Math.max(la, lb) === 2 && (a.includes(b) || b.includes(a));
    }

    let i = 0, j = 0, diffs = 0;
    while (i < la && j < lb) {
      if (a[i] === b[j]) {
        i++; j++;
      } else {
        diffs++;
        if (diffs > 1) return false;
        if (la > lb) i++;
        else if (lb > la) j++;
        else { i++; j++; }
      }
    }
    if (i < la || j < lb) diffs++;
    return diffs <= 1;
  }

  function isSoftOcrTokenMatch(strToken, queryToken) {
    if (!strToken || !queryToken) return false;
    if (strToken === queryToken) return true;
    if (strToken.includes(".") !== queryToken.includes(".")) return false;

    // Bắt buộc chữ cái đầu tiên khớp chính xác
    if (strToken[0] !== queryToken[0]) return false;

    // Không chạy mờ với từ quá ngắn (< 4 ký tự)
    if (strToken.length < 4 || queryToken.length < 4) return false;

    const digitsA = strToken.replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
    const digitsB = queryToken.replace(/[^0-9]/g, "");
    if (digitsA !== digitsB) return false;

    return isEditDistanceOne(strToken, queryToken);
  }

  async function executeSearch(options = {}) {
    const rawQuery = document.getElementById("ai-search-input").value;
    if (!rawQuery || !rawQuery.trim() || !S.currentFileId || !S.currentManifest) return;

    const preferPl01 = Boolean(options && options.preferPl01);
    const silentNotFound = Boolean(options && options.silentNotFound);

    S.lastSearchedQuery = rawQuery.trim();
    clearSearchState();

    const qTokens = rawQuery
      .replace(/(\d)[.,](?=\d)/g, "$1\uE001")
      .replace(/[\(\)\[\]\{\}\-\|\/\\,;:\.\?\!\<\>+=_]/g, " ")
      .replace(/\uE001/g, ".")
      .split(/\s+/)
      .map(cleanNaturalToken)
      .filter(Boolean);
    const numTokens = qTokens.length;
    if (numTokens === 0) return;

    const isCodeMode = isSingleTechnicalCode(rawQuery);
    const matches = [];

    function isOverlapping(m1, m2) {
      if (m1.page !== m2.page) return false;
      const b1 = m1.boxes ? m1.boxes[0] : m1;
      const b2 = m2.boxes ? m2.boxes[0] : m2;
      const yDiff = Math.abs(b1.y - b2.y);
      const maxHeight = Math.max(b1.h, b2.h);
      if (yDiff > maxHeight * 0.7) return false;

      const xOverlap = Math.min(b1.x + b1.w, b2.x + b2.w) - Math.max(b1.x, b2.x);
      const minWidth = Math.min(b1.w, b2.w);
      return xOverlap > (minWidth * 0.35);
    }

    function addMatch(newMatch) {
      newMatch.isCode = isCodeMode;
      for (let i = 0; i < matches.length; i++) {
        if (isOverlapping(matches[i], newMatch)) return;
      }
      matches.push(newMatch);
    }

    function isStrictTokenMatch(strToken, queryToken) {
      return strToken === queryToken;
    }
    function isPrefixTokenMatch(strToken, queryToken) {
      if (isStrictTokenMatch(strToken, queryToken)) return true;
      return strToken.startsWith(queryToken);
    }
    function isFuzzyTokenMatch(strToken, queryToken) {
      if (strToken === queryToken) return true;
      if (strToken.includes(".") !== queryToken.includes(".")) return false;
      const qLen = queryToken.length;
      const sLen = strToken.length;
      if (qLen >= 2 && /[0-9]/.test(queryToken) && /[0-9]/.test(strToken)) {
        if (qLen - sLen === 1 && (queryToken.endsWith(strToken) || queryToken.startsWith(strToken))) return true;
      }
      if (qLen >= 4 && sLen === qLen - 1 && queryToken.startsWith(strToken)) return true;
      return false;
    }
    function isFuzzyPrefixMatch(strToken, queryToken) {
      if (isFuzzyTokenMatch(strToken, queryToken)) return true;
      return strToken.startsWith(queryToken);
    }

    const totalPages = S.currentManifest.numPages || S.currentManifest.pages.length;
    const fallbackAppendixStart = Math.max(1, totalPages - 6);
    const appendixStart = S.currentManifest.appendixStartPage || fallbackAppendixStart;

    // Kiểm tra ranh giới cột bảng (chặn nối từ xuyên vách ngăn cột)
    function isCrossColumnBoundary(items, leftIt, rightIt, isAppendix) {
      const leftEnd = leftIt[1] + leftIt[3];
      const rightStart = rightIt[1];
      const gap = rightStart - leftEnd;
      const avgH = Math.max(6, (leftIt[4] + rightIt[4]) / 2);

      const hardMaxGap = isAppendix ? Math.max(18, avgH * 1.9) : 65;
      if (gap > hardMaxGap) return true;

      if (gap <= Math.max(3.5, avgH * 0.42)) return false;

      const midX = (leftEnd + rightStart) / 2;
      const yCenter = (leftIt[2] + rightIt[2]) / 2;

      let crossingWords = 0;
      let alignedRightColCount = 0;
      const distinctY = new Set();

      for (let idx = 0; idx < items.length; idx++) {
        const t = items[idx];
        if (!t[0] || !t[0].trim()) continue;

        const yDiff = Math.abs(t[2] - yCenter);
        if (yDiff > 5 && yDiff <= 120) {
          distinctY.add(Math.round(t[2] / 6));
          if (t[1] < midX - 1.5 && (t[1] + t[3]) > midX + 1.5) crossingWords++;
          if (Math.abs(t[1] - rightStart) <= 3.5) alignedRightColCount++;
        }
      }

      if (!isAppendix) {
        return (distinctY.size >= 2 && crossingWords === 0 && alignedRightColCount >= 2);
      }

      if (distinctY.size >= 2) {
        if (crossingWords === 0 && (alignedRightColCount >= 1 || gap > avgH * 0.85)) return true;
        if (crossingWords <= 1 && alignedRightColCount >= 2 && gap > avgH * 0.65) return true;
      }

      if (gap > Math.max(12, avgH * 1.25)) return true;

      return false;
    }

    S.currentManifest.pages.forEach(function(p) {
      if (!p.textItems || p.textItems.length === 0) return;

      const isAppendixPage = (p.pageNum >= appendixStart) || (p.textItems.length > 250);
      const matchedIndicesInA = new Set();

      if (numTokens === 1) {
        const targetWord = qTokens[0];
        p.textItems.forEach(function(it) {
          if (!it[0]) return;
          const pure = cleanNaturalToken(it[0]);
          if (!pure) return;
          const solidPure = pure.replace(/\s+/g, "");

          const isMatched = isAppendixPage
            ? isPrefixTokenMatch(solidPure, targetWord)
            : isFuzzyPrefixMatch(solidPure, targetWord);

          if (isMatched) {
            let widthRatio = 1;
            if (!isAppendixPage || !/[0-9]/.test(solidPure)) {
              widthRatio = targetWord.length / Math.max(1, solidPure.length);
            }
            const b = { x: it[1], y: it[2], w: Math.max(10, widthRatio * it[3]), h: it[4] };
            addMatch({
              page: p.pageNum, pageWidth: p.width, pageHeight: p.height,
              x: b.x, y: b.y, w: b.w, h: b.h, boxes: [b]
            });
          } else if (solidPure.length > targetWord.length) {
            let startIdx = 0;
            while ((startIdx = solidPure.indexOf(targetWord, startIdx)) !== -1) {
              const isNumeric = /[0-9]/.test(solidPure);
              const prevSolidChar = startIdx > 0 ? solidPure[startIdx - 1] : "";
              const beforeChar = startIdx > 0 ? it[0][startIdx - 1] : " ";
              const isStartOfWord = !(/[a-zA-Z]/.test(beforeChar));

              if ((isStartOfWord || isNumeric) && prevSolidChar !== ".") {
                const startRatio = startIdx / Math.max(1, solidPure.length);
                let widthRatio = targetWord.length / Math.max(1, solidPure.length);
                if (isAppendixPage && isNumeric && startIdx + targetWord.length === solidPure.length) {
                  widthRatio = 1 - startRatio;
                }
                const b = {
                  x: it[1] + (startRatio * it[3]),
                  y: it[2],
                  w: Math.max(14, widthRatio * it[3]),
                  h: it[4]
                };
                addMatch({
                  page: p.pageNum, pageWidth: p.width, pageHeight: p.height,
                  x: b.x, y: b.y, w: b.w, h: b.h, boxes: [b]
                });
              }
              startIdx += targetWord.length;
            }
          }
        });
      } else {
        const cleanQuery = cleanSentenceNatural(rawQuery);

        p.textItems.forEach(function(it, idx) {
          if (!it[0]) return;
          const itClean = cleanSentenceNatural(it[0]);
          let startPos = 0;
          while ((startPos = itClean.indexOf(cleanQuery, startPos)) !== -1) {
            const beforeChar = startPos > 0 ? itClean[startPos - 1] : " ";
            if (beforeChar === " ") {
              const startRatio = startPos / Math.max(1, itClean.length);
              const widthRatio = cleanQuery.length / Math.max(1, itClean.length);
              const b = { x: it[1] + (startRatio * it[3]), y: it[2], w: Math.max(20, widthRatio * it[3]), h: it[4] };
              addMatch({
                page: p.pageNum, pageWidth: p.width, pageHeight: p.height,
                x: b.x, y: b.y, w: b.w, h: b.h, boxes: [b]
              });
              matchedIndicesInA.add(idx);
            }
            startPos += cleanQuery.length;
          }
        });

        for (let i = 0; i < p.textItems.length; i++) {
          if (matchedIndicesInA.has(i)) continue;

          const firstIt = p.textItems[i];
          if (!firstIt[0]) continue;
          const firstClean = cleanNaturalToken(firstIt[0]).replace(/\s+/g, "");
          if (!firstClean) continue;

          let isFirstMatch = false;
          let firstIsFuzzy = false;
          let firstStartRatio = 0;

          if (isAppendixPage) {
            if (isStrictTokenMatch(firstClean, qTokens[0])) {
              isFirstMatch = true;
            } else if (firstClean.endsWith(qTokens[0]) && /[0-9]/.test(firstClean)) {
              isFirstMatch = true;
              firstStartRatio = firstClean.indexOf(qTokens[0]) / firstClean.length;
            }
          } else {
            if (isFuzzyTokenMatch(firstClean, qTokens[0])) {
              isFirstMatch = true;
            } else if (isSoftOcrTokenMatch(firstClean, qTokens[0])) {
              isFirstMatch = true;
              firstIsFuzzy = true;
            }
          }

          if (!isFirstMatch) continue;

          let matchedList = [{ it: firstIt, clean: firstClean, sR: firstStartRatio, isWrap: false }];
          let currentItemIdx = i;
          let sequenceValid = true;
          let lineBreakCount = 0;
          let fuzzyUsed = firstIsFuzzy ? 1 : 0;
          let strictCount = firstIsFuzzy ? 0 : 1;
          let maxStrictLen = firstIsFuzzy ? 0 : firstClean.length;
          const maxFuzzy = 1;

          for (let k = 1; k < numTokens; k++) {
            let foundNext = false;
            const isLastSearchToken = (k === numTokens - 1);
            const prevItem = matchedList[matchedList.length - 1].it;

            // 1. Quét tìm trên cùng một dòng ngang (Cập nhật mốc tọa độ qua dấu câu, khóa chặt ranh giới cột)
            let tempIdx = currentItemIdx;
            let lastSameLineIt = prevItem;
            const maxStep = 5;

            while (++tempIdx < p.textItems.length && tempIdx <= currentItemIdx + maxStep) {
              const it = p.textItems[tempIdx];
              const rawTrim = (it[0] || "").trim();
              const clean = cleanNaturalToken(it[0]).replace(/\s+/g, "");
              if (!clean) {
                if (!rawTrim) continue;
                if (rawTrim === "|") break;
                const pYDiff = Math.abs(it[2] - lastSameLineIt[2]);
                const pGap = it[1] - (lastSameLineIt[1] + lastSameLineIt[3]);
                const pAvgH = Math.max(6, (lastSameLineIt[4] + it[4]) / 2);
                const maxPunctGap = isAppendixPage ? Math.max(4.5, pAvgH * 0.6) : 12;
                if (pGap >= -5 && pGap <= maxPunctGap && pYDiff <= 6 && !isCrossColumnBoundary(p.textItems, lastSameLineIt, it, isAppendixPage)) {
                  lastSameLineIt = it;
                  continue;
                }
                break;
              }

              let isStrictOrPrefix = false;
              if (isAppendixPage) {
                isStrictOrPrefix = isLastSearchToken ? isPrefixTokenMatch(clean, qTokens[k]) : isStrictTokenMatch(clean, qTokens[k]);
              } else {
                isStrictOrPrefix = isLastSearchToken ? isFuzzyPrefixMatch(clean, qTokens[k]) : isFuzzyTokenMatch(clean, qTokens[k]);
              }

              const isSoft = !isAppendixPage && !isStrictOrPrefix && (fuzzyUsed < maxFuzzy) && isSoftOcrTokenMatch(clean, qTokens[k]);

              if (isStrictOrPrefix || isSoft) {
                const yDiff = Math.abs(it[2] - lastSameLineIt[2]);
                const gap = it[1] - (lastSameLineIt[1] + lastSameLineIt[3]);
                const avgH = Math.max(6, (lastSameLineIt[4] + it[4]) / 2);
                const bridgedViaPunct = (lastSameLineIt !== prevItem);
                const maxWordGap = (isAppendixPage && bridgedViaPunct) ? Math.max(5.5, avgH * 0.75) : Infinity;

                if (gap >= -5 && gap <= maxWordGap && yDiff <= 6 && !isCrossColumnBoundary(p.textItems, lastSameLineIt, it, isAppendixPage)) {
                  foundNext = true;
                  currentItemIdx = tempIdx;
                  matchedList.push({ it: it, clean: clean, sR: 0, isWrap: false });
                  if (!isSoft) {
                    strictCount++;
                    if (clean.length > maxStrictLen) maxStrictLen = clean.length;
                  } else {
                    fuzzyUsed++;
                  }
                  break;
                } else {
                  break;
                }
              } else {
                break;
              }
            }

            // 2. Ngoại lệ xuống dòng (chặn vách ngăn cột & chống nhảy cóc dòng, hỗ trợ khi OCR đảo thứ tự block)
            if (!foundNext && lineBreakCount < 2 && isAppendixPage) {
              const maxOffset = 120;
              const candidateIndices = [];
              for (let off = 1; off <= maxOffset; off++) {
                if (currentItemIdx + off < p.textItems.length) candidateIndices.push(currentItemIdx + off);
              }
              for (let off = 1; off <= maxOffset; off++) {
                if (currentItemIdx - off >= 0) candidateIndices.push(currentItemIdx - off);
              }

              for (let cIdx = 0; cIdx < candidateIndices.length; cIdx++) {
                tempIdx = candidateIndices[cIdx];
                const it = p.textItems[tempIdx];
                const clean = cleanNaturalToken(it[0]).replace(/\s+/g, "");
                if (!clean) continue;

                const isStrictOrPrefix = isLastSearchToken ? isPrefixTokenMatch(clean, qTokens[k]) : isStrictTokenMatch(clean, qTokens[k]);
                const isSoft = !isAppendixPage && !isStrictOrPrefix && (fuzzyUsed < maxFuzzy) && isSoftOcrTokenMatch(clean, qTokens[k]);

                if (isStrictOrPrefix || isSoft) {
                  const yDrop = prevItem[2] - it[2];
                  const isWrapLeft = it[1] < (prevItem[1] + prevItem[3] - 10);

                  const scanMin = Math.max(0, Math.min(currentItemIdx, tempIdx) - 35);
                  const scanMax = Math.min(p.textItems.length - 1, Math.max(currentItemIdx, tempIdx) + 35);

                  let isLastWordOfLine1 = true;
                  for (let m = scanMin; m <= scanMax; m++) {
                    if (m === currentItemIdx) continue;
                    const midIt = p.textItems[m];
                    const midClean = cleanNaturalToken(midIt[0]);
                    if (!midClean) continue;

                    const gapRight = midIt[1] - (prevItem[1] + prevItem[3]);
                    if (Math.abs(midIt[2] - prevItem[2]) <= 4 && gapRight >= -3 && gapRight <= 8.5 && !isCrossColumnBoundary(p.textItems, prevItem, midIt, isAppendixPage)) {
                      isLastWordOfLine1 = false;
                      break;
                    }
                  }

                  let isFirstWordOfLine2 = true;
                  for (let prevIdx = scanMax; prevIdx >= scanMin; prevIdx--) {
                    if (prevIdx === tempIdx) continue;
                    const pIt = p.textItems[prevIdx];
                    const pClean = cleanNaturalToken(pIt[0]);
                    if (!pClean) continue;

                    const isSttFormat = /^(?:[0-9]{1,4}|[a-z]|[ivx]{1,5})$/i.test(pClean);
                    const isLeftMargin = pIt[1] < (p.width * 0.25);
                    if (isSttFormat && isLeftMargin) continue;

                    const gapLeft = it[1] - (pIt[1] + pIt[3]);
                    if (Math.abs(pIt[2] - it[2]) <= 4 && gapLeft >= -5 && gapLeft <= 15 && !isCrossColumnBoundary(p.textItems, pIt, it, isAppendixPage)) {
                      isFirstWordOfLine2 = false;
                      break;
                    }
                  }

                  const lineH = Math.max(prevItem[4] || 0, it[4] || 0, 8);
                  const maxYDrop = isAppendixPage ? Math.max(22, lineH * 2.4) : 45;

                  if (yDrop >= 3 && yDrop <= maxYDrop && isWrapLeft && isLastWordOfLine1 && isFirstWordOfLine2) {
                    let hasInterveningLine = false;
                    const colLeft = Math.min(it[1], prevItem[1]) - 10;
                    const colRight = Math.max(it[1] + it[3], prevItem[1] + prevItem[3]) + 10;
                    for (let interIdx = scanMin; interIdx <= scanMax; interIdx++) {
                      const interIt = p.textItems[interIdx];
                      if (!interIt[0] || !interIt[0].trim()) continue;
                      const interLeft = interIt[1];
                      const interRight = interIt[1] + interIt[3];
                      if (interRight < colLeft || interLeft > colRight) continue;
                      if (interIt[2] < prevItem[2] - 5 && interIt[2] > it[2] + 5) {
                        hasInterveningLine = true;
                        break;
                      }
                    }

                    if (!hasInterveningLine) {
                      foundNext = true;
                      lineBreakCount++;
                      currentItemIdx = tempIdx;
                      matchedList.push({ it: it, clean: clean, sR: 0, isWrap: true });
                      if (!isSoft) {
                        strictCount++;
                        if (clean.length > maxStrictLen) maxStrictLen = clean.length;
                      } else {
                        fuzzyUsed++;
                      }
                      break;
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }

            if (!foundNext) {
              sequenceValid = false;
              break;
            }
          }

          const isPairSafe = (numTokens > 2) || (fuzzyUsed === 0) || (maxStrictLen >= 2);

          if (sequenceValid && matchedList.length === numTokens && strictCount >= 1 && fuzzyUsed <= maxFuzzy && isPairSafe) {
            const lines = [];
            let currentLine = [];
            matchedList.forEach(item => {
              if (item.isWrap && currentLine.length > 0) {
                lines.push(currentLine);
                currentLine = [];
              }
              currentLine.push(item);
            });
            if (currentLine.length > 0) lines.push(currentLine);

            const boxes = lines.map((lineTokens, lIdx) => {
              const isLastLine = (lIdx === lines.length - 1);
              const startItem = lineTokens[0];
              const endItem = lineTokens[lineTokens.length - 1];

              const exactX = startItem.it[1] + (startItem.sR * startItem.it[3]);
              let exactEndX = endItem.it[1] + endItem.it[3];

              if (isLastLine) {
                const lastTargetToken = qTokens[numTokens - 1];
                let endRatio = Math.min(1, lastTargetToken.length / Math.max(1, endItem.clean.length));
                if (isAppendixPage && /[0-9]/.test(endItem.clean)) endRatio = 1;
                exactEndX = endItem.it[1] + (endRatio * endItem.it[3]);
              }

              const minY = Math.min(...lineTokens.map(t => t.it[2]));
              const maxH = Math.max(...lineTokens.map(t => t.it[4]));

              return { x: exactX, y: minY, w: Math.max(16, exactEndX - exactX), h: maxH };
            });

            addMatch({
              page: p.pageNum, pageWidth: p.width, pageHeight: p.height,
              x: boxes[0].x, y: boxes[0].y, w: boxes[0].w, h: boxes[0].h, boxes: boxes
            });
          }
        }
      }

      if (isCodeMode) {
        const targetCode = cleanCodeToken(rawQuery);
        for (let i = 0; i < p.textItems.length - 1; i++) {
          const it1 = p.textItems[i];
          const it2 = p.textItems[i + 1];
          const c1 = cleanCodeToken(it1[0]);
          const c2 = cleanCodeToken(it2[0]);

          const gap = it2[1] - (it1[1] + it1[3]);
          const yDiff = Math.abs(it2[2] - it1[2]);

          if (gap >= -3 && gap <= 14 && yDiff <= 5 && (c1 + c2 === targetCode) && !isCrossColumnBoundary(p.textItems, it1, it2, isAppendixPage)) {
            let startX = it1[1];
            if (it1[0].startsWith("(") || it1[0].startsWith("[")) startX += (it1[3] * 0.18);
            let endX = it2[1] + it2[3];
            if (it2[0].endsWith(")") || it2[0].endsWith("]") || it2[0].includes(" ")) {
              endX = it2[1] + (it2[3] * (c2.length / Math.max(c2.length, it2[0].length)));
            }

            const b = { x: startX, y: Math.min(it1[2], it2[2]), w: Math.max(14, endX - startX), h: Math.max(it1[4], it2[4]) };
            addMatch({
              page: p.pageNum, pageWidth: p.width, pageHeight: p.height,
              x: b.x, y: b.y, w: b.w, h: b.h, boxes: [b]
            });
            i++;
          }
        }
      }
    });

    if (matches.length > 0) {
      S.searchResults = matches;
      onSearchReady(preferPl01);
      return matches;
    } else {
      document.getElementById("search-counter").textContent = "0/0";
      if (!silentNotFound) {
        if (typeof showToast_PL === "function") {
          showToast_PL('Không tìm thấy từ khóa: "' + rawQuery + '"', "error");
        } else {
          alert('Không tìm thấy từ khóa: "' + rawQuery + '"');
        }
      }
      return [];
    }
  }

  function handleSearchSubmit(isShift = false) {
    const currentInput = document.getElementById("ai-search-input").value.trim();
    if (currentInput === S.lastSearchedQuery && S.searchResults.length > 0) {
      navigateMatch(isShift ? -1 : 1);
    } else {
      executeSearch();
    }
  }

  function handleSearchKey(e) {
    if (e.key === "Enter") {
      e.preventDefault();
      handleSearchSubmit(e.shiftKey);
    }
  }

  function onSearchReady(preferPl01 = false) {
    document.getElementById("btn-prev").disabled = false;
    document.getElementById("btn-next").disabled = false;
    redrawAllHighlights();

    let startIdx = 0;
    if (preferPl01 && S.currentManifest && S.currentManifest.appendixStartPage) {
      const apStart = S.currentManifest.appendixStartPage;
      const pl01Idx = S.searchResults.findIndex(m => m.page >= apStart);
      if (pl01Idx !== -1) startIdx = pl01Idx;
    }

    S.currentMatchIndex = startIdx;
    updateNavigationUI();
    jumpToMatch(S.currentMatchIndex);
  }

  function redrawAllHighlights() {
    document.querySelectorAll(".ai-highlight-box").forEach(el => el.remove());

    function docPx(n) {
      return "calc(" + (Math.round(n * 100) / 100) + "px * var(--doc-scale))";
    }

    S.searchResults.forEach((m, idx) => {
      const pageEl = document.getElementById("page-wrapper-" + m.page);
      if (!pageEl) return;

      const rightPad = m.isCode ? 3 : 2;
      const boxes = (m.boxes && m.boxes.length > 0) ? m.boxes : [m];
      const isMultiLine = boxes.length > 1;

      boxes.forEach((box, bIdx) => {
        const highlight = document.createElement("div");
        highlight.id = "highlight-match-" + idx + "-" + bIdx;

        let wrapClass = "";
        if (isMultiLine) {
          if (bIdx === 0) wrapClass = " wrap-start";
          else if (bIdx === boxes.length - 1) wrapClass = " wrap-end";
        }

        highlight.className = "ai-highlight-box match-group-" + idx + wrapClass + (idx === S.currentMatchIndex ? " active-match" : "");
        highlight.style.left = docPx(box.x - 1.5);
        highlight.style.top = docPx((m.pageHeight - box.y - box.h) - 1);
        highlight.style.width = docPx(box.w + 1.5 + rightPad);
        highlight.style.height = docPx(box.h + 2);
        pageEl.appendChild(highlight);
      });
    });
  }

  function jumpToMatch(index) {
    document.querySelectorAll(".ai-highlight-box").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("active-match"));
    const match = S.searchResults[index];
    if (!match) return;

    const pageEl = document.getElementById("page-wrapper-" + match.page);
    if (!pageEl) return;
    document.querySelectorAll(".match-group-" + index).forEach(el => el.classList.add("active-match"));

    BV.ui.buildTextLayerForWrapper(pageEl);
    const targetCanvas = pageEl.querySelector("canvas.page-img");
    const targetPdfPage = parseInt(pageEl.dataset.pdfPage || match.page, 10);
    if (targetCanvas && !U.scalesEqual(targetCanvas.dataset.renderedScale, S.currentScale)) {
      BV.ui.renderPageToCanvas(targetCanvas, targetPdfPage);
    }

    const vp = document.getElementById("viewport-container");
    const mark = document.getElementById("highlight-match-" + index + "-0");
    const markTop = (mark && mark.offsetParent === pageEl) ? mark.offsetTop : 0;
    const markH = (mark && mark.offsetParent === pageEl) ? mark.offsetHeight : 0;
    const dest = pageEl.offsetTop + markTop - (vp.clientHeight - markH) / 2;
    const maxTop = Math.max(0, vp.scrollHeight - vp.clientHeight);
    vp.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, Math.min(dest, maxTop)), behavior: "smooth" });
    BV.ui.updatePageIndicatorUI(match.page);
  }

  function navigateMatch(direction) {
    if (S.searchResults.length === 0) return;
    S.currentMatchIndex += direction;
    if (S.currentMatchIndex >= S.searchResults.length) S.currentMatchIndex = 0;
    if (S.currentMatchIndex < 0) S.currentMatchIndex = S.searchResults.length - 1;
    updateNavigationUI();
    jumpToMatch(S.currentMatchIndex);
  }

  function updateNavigationUI() {
    const counter = document.getElementById("search-counter");
    if (counter) counter.textContent = S.searchResults.length > 0 ? `${S.currentMatchIndex + 1}/${S.searchResults.length}` : "0/0";
  }

  function clearSearchState() {
    S.searchResults = [];
    S.currentMatchIndex = -1;
    document.querySelectorAll(".ai-highlight-box").forEach(el => el.remove());
    const btnP = document.getElementById("btn-prev"), btnN = document.getElementById("btn-next");
    if (btnP) btnP.disabled = true;
    if (btnN) btnN.disabled = true;
    const counter = document.getElementById("search-counter");
    if (counter) counter.textContent = "0/0";
  }

  let isSearchShortcutBound = false;
  function setupSearchShortcut() {
    if (isSearchShortcutBound) return;
    isSearchShortcutBound = true;

    window.addEventListener("keydown", function(e) {
      if ((e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey) && (e.key === "f" || e.key === "F")) {
        if (!S.currentFileId && !S.currentManifest) return;

        const panel = document.getElementById("pl-dock-preview-panel");
        const vp = document.getElementById("viewport-container");
        const isViewerActive = (panel && panel.offsetParent !== null) || (vp && vp.offsetParent !== null);
        if (!isViewerActive) return;

        const input = document.getElementById("ai-search-input");
        if (!input) return;

        e.preventDefault();
        input.focus();
        input.select();
      }
    });
  }

  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", setupSearchShortcut);
  } else {
    setupSearchShortcut();
  }

  BV.search = {
    isSingleTechnicalCode,
    cleanCodeToken,
    cleanNaturalToken,
    cleanSentenceNatural,
    isEditDistanceOne,
    isSoftOcrTokenMatch,
    executeSearch,
    handleSearchSubmit,
    handleSearchKey,
    onSearchReady,
    redrawAllHighlights,
    jumpToMatch,
    navigateMatch,
    updateNavigationUI,
    clearSearchState,
    setupSearchShortcut
  };
})();

// BCONS DOCUMENT VIEWER - MODULE 1/4: STATE, STORAGE, GZIP & PROGRESS UI

(() => {
  if (typeof pdfjsLib !== "undefined" && pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions) {
    pdfjsLib.GlobalWorkerOptions.workerSrc = "https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdf.js/3.11.174/pdf.worker.min.js";
  }

  const BV = (window.__BconsViewer = window.__BconsViewer || {});

  // =========================================================================
  // CẤU HÌNH & CẦU NỐI BACKEND BCONS-VIEWER (CLOUD VISION AI)
  // =========================================================================
  const GAS_WEB_APP_URL = "https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbwlhByeOZIdDs4dIpioMo4e-2On3ad317kjoFMobpPiNUjtYi3o8Aa_jCXuoUWRucVJ/exec";

  function createGasBridge(onSuccess, onFailure) {
    return new Proxy({}, {
      get: function(_, prop) {
        if (prop === "withSuccessHandler") {
          return function(fn) { return createGasBridge(fn, onFailure); };
        }
        if (prop === "withFailureHandler") {
          return function(fn) { return createGasBridge(onSuccess, fn); };
        }
        return function(...args) {
          const payload = args.length === 1 ? args[0] : args;
          callBackend(prop, payload)
            .then(data => {
              if (onSuccess) onSuccess(data);
            })
            .catch(err => {
              if (onFailure) onFailure(err);
            });
        };
      }
    });
  }

  const googleScriptRun = (typeof window.google !== "undefined" && window.google.script && window.google.script.run)
    ? window.google.script.run
    : createGasBridge(null, null);

  // =========================================================================
  // HẰNG SỐ & STATE DÙNG CHUNG CHO TOÀN BỘ VIEWER
  // =========================================================================
  const DB_NAME = "Bcons_Prv_Cache_DB";
  const PAGE_STORE = "page_bitmaps";
  const PDF_STORE = "pdf_binaries";
  const STORE_NAME = "manifests_v13_vision";
  const PL01_INDEX_STORE = "pl01_indexes";
  const MAX_LOCAL_FILES = 3;
  const MAX_RAM_FILES = 5;

  const S = (BV.state = {
    LARGE_FILE_THRESHOLD_MB: 6.0,
    currentManifest: null,
    currentFileId: null,
    currentScale: 1.0,
    baseFitScale: 1.0,
    isDragging: false,
    startX: 0,
    startY: 0,
    scrollLeftStart: 0,
    scrollTopStart: 0,

    searchResults: [],
    currentMatchIndex: -1,
    pageObserver: null,
    lastSearchedQuery: "",

    dbPromise: null,

    SESSION_LRU_KEYS: [],
    SESSION_MANIFEST_CACHE: {},

    currentSessionEpoch: 0,
    activeFetchAbortController: null,
    currentFileName: "",

    driveTokenExpiresAt: 0,
